Project\\A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse\\images\\cinema-default.svg"}},{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"/* ==========================================================================\n   CineVerse - Main Stylesheet\n   Semantic layout using CSS3 Flexbox, Grid, and responsive media queries.\n   ========================================================================== */\n\n/* 1. CSS Custom Properties (Theme Variables) */\n:root {\n  --color-bg: #0b0f19;\n  --color-bg-elevated: #121826;\n  --color-surface: #161f33;\n  --color-surface-hover: #1d2842;\n  --color-border: #26324d;\n  --color-text: #f1f5f9;\n  --color-text-muted: #94a3b8;\n  --color-primary: #e50914;\n  --color-primary-hover: #f41b26;\n  --color-accent: #f5c518;\n  --color-success: #10b981;\n\n  --radius-sm: 8px;\n  --radius-md: 12px;\n  --radius-lg: 18px;\n  --shadow-card: 0 12px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);\n  --transition-fast: 0.2s ease;\n  --max-width: 1200px;\n}\n\n/* 2. Base Reset & Typography */\n*,\n*::before,\n*::after {\n  box-sizing: border-box;\n  margin: 0;\n  padding: 0;\n}\n\nhtml {\n  scroll-behavior: smooth;\n}\n\nbody {\n  font-family: \"Segoe UI\", system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, Roboto, sans-serif;\n  background-color: var(--color-bg);\n  color: var(--color-text);\n  line-height: 1.6;\n  min-height: 100vh;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n}\n\nimg {\n  max-width: 100%;\n  display: block;\n}\n\na {\n  color: inherit;\n  text-decoration: none;\n}\n\nbutton,\nselect {\n  font-family: inherit;\n}\n\n.container {\n  width: 100%;\n  max-width: var(--max-width);\n  margin: 0 auto;\n  padding: 0 1.25rem;\n}\n\n.sr-only {\n  position: absolute;\n  width: 1px;\n  height: 1px;\n  padding: 0;\n  margin: -1px;\n  overflow: hidden;\n  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);\n  white-space: nowrap;\n  border: 0;\n}\n\n/* 3. Header & Responsive Navigation (Flexbox + Mobile Hamburger) */\n.site-header {\n  background-color: rgba(11, 15, 25, 0.94);\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  position: sticky;\n  top: 0;\n  z-index: 100;\n  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);\n}\n\n.navbar {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  min-height: 70px;\n  position: relative;\n}\n\n.brand-logo {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.65rem;\n  font-size: 1.35rem;\n  font-weight: 800;\n  letter-spacing: 0.5px;\n}\n\n.brand-badge {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  width: 36px;\n  height: 36px;\n  border-radius: 10px;\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-primary), #b20710);\n  color: #ffffff;\n  font-size: 1.05rem;\n  font-weight: 900;\n}\n\n.brand-highlight {\n  color: var(--color-accent);\n}\n\n/* Hamburger button (visible on mobile) */\n.hamburger-btn {\n  display: none;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  justify-content: center;\n  gap: 5px;\n  width: 42px;\n  height: 42px;\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\n  cursor: pointer;\n  padding: 0 9px;\n}\n\n.hamburger-line {\n  width: 100%;\n  height: 2px;\n  background-color: var(--color-text);\n  border-radius: 2px;\n  transition: transform var(--transition-fast), opacity var(--transition-fast);\n}\n\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\"true\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(1) {\n  transform: translateY(7px) rotate(45deg);\n}\n\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\"true\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(2) {\n  opacity: 0;\n}\n\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\"true\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(3) {\n  transform: translateY(-7px) rotate(-45deg);\n}\n\n.nav-links {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  list-style: none;\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n}\n\n.nav-link {\n  display: inline-block;\n  padding: 0.5rem 0.9rem;\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n  font-weight: 500;\n  transition: color var(--transition-fast), background-color var(--transition-fast);\n}\n\n.nav-link:hover,\n.nav-link:focus-visible {\n  color: var(--color-text);\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\n}\n\n.nav-link.active {\n  color: #ffffff;\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\n}\n\n/* 4. Main Content & Booking Progress Stepper */\n.main-content {\n  flex: 1;\n  padding: 2rem 0 3.5rem;\n}\n\n.booking-steps {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.75rem;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  margin-bottom: 1.75rem;\n  padding: 0.9rem 1.1rem;\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\n}\n\n.step-item {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n  font-size: 0.92rem;\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n}\n\n.step-number {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  width: 26px;\n  height: 26px;\n  border-radius: 50%;\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.step-item.active {\n  color: var(--color-text);\n  font-weight: 600;\n}\n\n.step-item.active .step-number {\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\n  border-color: var(--color-primary);\n  color: #ffffff;\n}\n\n.step-item.completed {\n  color: var(--color-success);\n}\n\n.step-item.completed .step-number {\n  background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.18);\n  border-color: var(--color-success);\n  color: var(--color-success);\n}\n\n.step-divider {\n  color: var(--color-border);\n}\n\n/* 5. Page Hero & Filter Bar */\n.page-hero {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  align-items: flex-end;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 1.5rem;\n  margin-bottom: 1.75rem;\n}\n\n.hero-text h1 {\n  font-size: clamp(1.65rem, 3vw, 2.3rem);\n  line-height: 1.2;\n  margin-bottom: 0.45rem;\n}\n\n.hero-text p {\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n  max-width: 620px;\n}\n\n.city-filter-box {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  gap: 0.4rem;\n  min-width: 230px;\n}\n\n.city-filter-label {\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  color: var(--color-accent);\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 0.5px;\n}\n\n.city-select {\n  appearance: none;\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\n  color: var(--color-text);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\n  padding: 0.7rem 2.25rem 0.7rem 0.95rem;\n  font-size: 1rem;\n  font-weight: 500;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  background-image: linear-gradient(45deg, transparent 50%, var(--color-text-muted) 50%),\n    linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-text-muted) 50%, transparent 50%);\n  background-position: calc(100% - 18px) 50%, calc(100% - 12px) 50%;\n  background-size: 6px 6px, 6px 6px;\n  background-repeat: no-repeat;\n  transition: border-color var(--transition-fast);\n}\n\n.city-select:hover,\n.city-select:focus {\n  outline: none;\n  border-color: var(--color-accent);\n}\n\n/* Results summary & saved selection banner */\n.results-toolbar {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 0.75rem;\n  margin-bottom: 1.25rem;\n}\n\n.results-count {\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n}\n\n.saved-selection-pill {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  gap: 0.5rem;\n  padding: 0.4rem 0.85rem;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  background-color: rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.12);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.35);\n  color: var(--color-accent);\n  font-size: 0.88rem;\n}\n\n/* 6. Cinema Cards Grid (CSS Grid) */\n.cinema-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));\n  gap: 1.5rem;\n}\n\n.cinema-card {\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\n  overflow: hidden;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-card);\n  transition: transform var(--transition-fast), border-color var(--transition-fast),\n    background-color var(--transition-fast);\n}\n\n.cinema-card:hover {\n  transform: translateY(-4px);\n  border-color: var(--color-accent);\n  background-color: var(--color-surface-hover);\n}\n\n.cinema-card.is-selected {\n  border-color: var(--color-primary);\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(229, 9, 20, 0.35), var(--shadow-card);\n}\n\n.cinema-card-media {\n  position: relative;\n  aspect-ratio: 16 / 9;\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\n  overflow: hidden;\n}\n\n.cinema-card-media img {\n  width: 100%;\n  height: 100%;\n  object-fit: cover;\n}\n\n.distance-badge {\n  position: absolute;\n  top: 0.75rem;\n  right: 0.75rem;\n  background-color: rgba(11, 15, 25, 0.88);\n  color: var(--color-accent);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.4);\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.65rem;\n  border-radius: 999px;\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n}\n\n.cinema-card-body {\n  padding: 1.2rem;\n  display: flex;\n  flex-direction: column;\n  flex: 1;\n  gap: 0.8rem;\n}\n\n.cinema-card-header {\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: flex-start;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 0.75rem;\n}\n\n.cinema-title {\n  font-size: 1.18rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  line-height: 1.3;\n}\n\n.cinema-rating {\n  flex-shrink: 0;\n  background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.14);\n  color: var(--color-success);\n  border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);\n  padding: 0.18rem 0.5rem;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  font-size: 0.82rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n}\n\n.cinema-location {\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n  font-size: 0.92rem;\n}\n\n.cinema-meta {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  gap: 0.45rem;\n}\n\n.feature-tag {\n  font-size: 0.78rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  padding: 0.22rem 0.55rem;\n  border-radius: 6px;\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  color: #cbd5e1;\n}\n\n.cinema-card-footer {\n  margin-top: auto;\n  padding-top: 0.65rem;\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  display: flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 0.75rem;\n}\n\n.screens-info {\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n}\n\n/* 7. Buttons */\n.btn {\n  display: inline-flex;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: center;\n  gap: 0.4rem;\n  padding: 0.65rem 1.15rem;\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\n  border: none;\n  font-size: 0.94rem;\n  font-weight: 600;\n  cursor: pointer;\n  transition: background-color var(--transition-fast), transform var(--transition-fast);\n}\n\n.btn-primary {\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\n  color: #ffffff;\n}\n\n.btn-primary:hover,\n.btn-primary:focus-visible {\n  background-color: var(--color-primary-hover);\n}\n\n.btn-secondary {\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\n  color: var(--color-text);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n}\n\n.btn-secondary:hover,\n.btn-secondary:focus-visible {\n  background-color: var(--color-surface-hover);\n}\n\n/* 8. Home Landing & Starter Page Sections */\n.landing-banner {\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #161f33 0%, #0d1322 65%, #2a0c13 100%);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);\n  padding: 2.5rem 2rem;\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: 1.4fr 1fr;\n  gap: 2rem;\n  align-items: center;\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\n}\n\n.landing-eyebrow {\n  display: inline-block;\n  color: var(--color-accent);\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\n  font-weight: 700;\n  text-transform: uppercase;\n  letter-spacing: 1px;\n  margin-bottom: 0.6rem;\n}\n\n.landing-banner h1 {\n  font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.6vw, 2.75rem);\n  line-height: 1.15;\n  margin-bottom: 0.85rem;\n}\n\n.landing-banner p {\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n  margin-bottom: 1.4rem;\n  max-width: 56ch;\n}\n\n.landing-actions {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  gap: 0.85rem;\n}\n\n.info-panel {\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\n  padding: 1.5rem;\n}\n\n.info-panel h2 {\n  font-size: 1.25rem;\n  margin-bottom: 0.65rem;\n}\n\n.info-panel p {\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n}\n\n.starter-grid {\n  display: grid;\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));\n  gap: 1.25rem;\n  margin-top: 1.25rem;\n}\n\n.empty-state {\n  grid-column: 1 / -1;\n  text-align: center;\n  padding: 2.5rem 1.5rem;\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\n  border: 1px dashed var(--color-border);\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n}\n\n/* 9. Footer */\n.site-footer {\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n  padding: 1.4rem 0;\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\n}\n\n.footer-inner {\n  display: flex;\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\n  align-items: center;\n  justify-content: space-between;\n  gap: 0.75rem;\n}\n\n/* 10. Responsive Breakpoints (Tablet & Mobile) */\n@media (max-width: 992px) {\n  .cinema-grid {\n    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));\n  }\n\n  .landing-banner {\n    grid-template-columns: 1fr;\n  }\n}\n\n@media (max-width: 768px) {\n  .hamburger-btn {\n    display: flex;\n  }\n\n  .nav-links {\n    display: none;\n    position: absolute;\n    top: 70px;\n    left: 0;\n    right: 0;\n    flex-direction: column;\n    align-items: stretch;\n    background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\n    border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\n    border-radius: var(--radius-md);\n    padding: 0.75rem;\n    box-shadow: var(--shadow-card);\n  }\n\n  .nav-links.is-open {\n    display: flex;\n  }\n\n  .nav-link {\n    width: 100%;\n    padding: 0.75rem 1rem;\n  }\n\n  .cinema-grid,\n  .starter-grid {\n    grid-template-columns: 1fr;\n  }\n\n  .city-filter-box {\n    width: 100%;\n  }\n}\n","Overwrite":true,"Path":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Desktop\\IICT Project\\A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse\\css\\style.css"}}]}

{"step_index":13,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T12:16:29Z","model":"gemini-4-hydrogen-high","input_tokens":10356,"cache_read_tokens":13339,"output_tokens":7768,"thinking":"Structuring the core application assets requires defining the central state manager script alongside the primary view templates. Initializing cinematic data arrays to dynamically render venue cards for default regions like Islamabad ensures the layout maps cleanly against the specified interactive flow.\n\n","tool_calls":[{"name":"update_steps","args":{"steps":[{"status":"done","title":"Create images and base stylesheets"},{"status":"in_progress","title":"Implement booking script and HTML pages"},{"status":"pending","title":"Add documentation log and verify files"}]}},{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"/**\n * CineVerse - Booking Flow & Cinema Selection Logic\n * Pure vanilla JavaScript for navigation, dynamic cinema rendering,\n * city filtering, and persisting booking state in localStorage.\n */\n\n(function () {\n  \"use strict\";\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 1. Constants & localStorage Keys\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  const DEFAULT_CITY = \"Islamabad\";\n  const STORAGE_KEYS = {\n    BOOKING_STATE: \"cineverse_booking_state\",\n    SELECTED_CITY: \"cineverse_selected_city\",\n    SELECTED_CINEMA: \"cineverse_selected_cinema\"\n  };\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 2. Cinema Data Array (rendered dynamically onto select-cinema.html)\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  const CINEMAS_DATA = [\n    {\n      id: \"cineplex-blue-area\",\n      name: \"Cineplex Blue Area\",\n      city: \"Islamabad\",\n      area: \"Jinnah Avenue, Blue Area\",\n      distanceKm: 2.4,\n      distanceText: \"2.4 km away\",\n      screens: 5,\n      rating: \"4.7\",\n      features: [\"4K Laser\", \"Dolby Digital\", \"Snack Bar\"],\n      imageFile: \"cineplex-blue-area.svg\"\n    },\n    {\n      id: \"centaurus-cinema\",\n      name: \"Centaurus Cinema\",\n      city: \"Islamabad\",\n      area: \"The Centaurus Mall, Sector F-8\",\n      distanceKm: 4.1,\n      distanceText: \"4.1 km away\",\n      screens: 6,\n      rating: \"4.8\",\n      features: [\"IMAX\", \"Dolby Atmos\", \"VIP Lounge\"],\n      imageFile: \"centaurus-cinema.svg\"\n    },\n    {\n      id: \"f9-arena\",\n      name: \"F-9 Arena\",\n      city: \"Islamabad\",\n      area: \"Fatima Jinnah Park, Sector F-9\",\n      distanceKm: 5.8,\n      distanceText: \"5.8 km away\",\n      screens: 4,\n      rating: \"4.6\",\n      features: [\"3D Ready\", \"Recliner Seats\", \"Free Parking\"],\n      imageFile: \"f9-arena.svg\"\n    },\n    {\n      id: \"bahria-cinegold-rwp\",\n      name: \"CineGold Plex Rawalpindi\",\n      city: \"Rawalpindi\",\n      area: \"Bahria Town Phase 7\",\n      distanceKm: 16.5,\n      distanceText: \"16.5 km away\",\n      screens: 5,\n      rating: \"4.5\",\n      features: [\"Gold Lounge\", \"Dolby Atmos\", \"3D\"],\n      imageFile: \"cinema-default.svg\"\n    },\n    {\n      id: \"cue-cinemas-lhr\",\n      name: \"Cue Cinemas Gulberg\",\n      city: \"Lahore\",\n      area: \"Main Boulevard, Gulberg III\",\n      distanceKm: 3.2,\n      distanceText: \"3.2 km away\",\n      screens: 7,\n      rating: \"4.8\",\n      features: [\"IMAX\", \"4K Projection\", \"Valet Parking\"],\n      imageFile: \"cinema-default.svg\"\n    },\n    {\n      id: \"nueplex-khi\",\n      name: \"Nueplex Cinemas DHA\",\n      city: \"Karachi\",\n      area: \"Khayaban-e-Jami, DHA Phase 8\",\n      distanceKm: 4.9,\n      distanceText: \"4.9 km away\",\n      screens: 8,\n      rating: \"4.7\",\n      features: [\"Ultra Screen\", \"Dolby Atmos\", \"Cafe\"],\n      imageFile: \"cinema-default.svg\"\n    }\n  ];\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 3. Helper Functions (Paths & localStorage)\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  /**\n   * Resolves the correct relative image folder depending on whether the\n   * current page lives inside `/pages/` or at the project root.\n   */\n  function getImageBasePath() {\n    return window.location.pathname.includes(\"/pages/\") ? \"../images/\" : \"images/\";\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Safely reads the saved booking object from localStorage.\n   */\n  function getSavedBookingState() {\n    try {\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE);\n      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\"Unable to read booking state from localStorage:\", error);\n      return null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Saves the selected city and cinema details to localStorage.\n   */\n  function saveSelectedCinema(city, cinema) {\n    const existingState = getSavedBookingState() || {};\n    const updatedState = {\n      ...existingState,\n      city: city,\n      cinemaId: cinema.id,\n      cinemaName: cinema.name,\n      cinemaArea: cinema.area,\n      distanceText: cinema.distanceText,\n      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()\n    };\n\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE, JSON.stringify(updatedState));\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CITY, city);\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CINEMA, cinema.name);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\"Unable to save selection to localStorage:\", error);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 4. Shared Mobile Hamburger Menu\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  function initMobileMenu() {\n    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById(\"hamburgerBtn\");\n    const navMenu = document.getElementById(\"primaryNav\");\n\n    if (!hamburgerBtn || !navMenu) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    function setMenuOpen(isOpen) {\n      hamburgerBtn.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", String(isOpen));\n      navMenu.classList.toggle(\"is-open\", isOpen);\n    }\n\n    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n      const currentlyExpanded = hamburgerBtn.getAttribute(\"aria-expanded\") === \"true\";\n      setMenuOpen(!currentlyExpanded);\n    });\n\n    // Close menu when a nav link is clicked on small screens\n    navMenu.querySelectorAll(\".nav-link\").forEach(function (link) {\n      link.addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n        setMenuOpen(false);\n      });\n    });\n\n    // Close menu with Escape key for keyboard accessibility\n    document.addEventListener(\"keydown\", function (event) {\n      if (event.key === \"Escape\") {\n        setMenuOpen(false);\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 5. Select Cinema Page (pages/select-cinema.html)\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  /**\n   * Builds a single semantic <article> card element for a cinema object.\n   */\n  function createCinemaCard(cinema, savedCinemaId, onSelectCinema) {\n    const card = document.createElement(\"article\");\n    card.className = \"cinema-card\";\n    if (savedCinemaId === cinema.id) {\n      card.classList.add(\"is-selected\");\n    }\n\n    // Media wrapper with cinema illustration & distance badge\n    const media = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    media.className = \"cinema-card-media\";\n\n    const img = document.createElement(\"img\");\n    img.src = getImageBasePath() + cinema.imageFile;\n    img.alt = cinema.name + \" cinema hall\";\n    img.loading = \"lazy\";\n\n    const distanceBadge = document.createElement(\"span\");\n    distanceBadge.className = \"distance-badge\";\n    distanceBadge.textContent = cinema.distanceText;\n\n    media.append(img, distanceBadge);\n\n    // Card content body\n    const body = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    body.className = \"cinema-card-body\";\n\n    const header = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    header.className = \"cinema-card-header\";\n\n    const title = document.createElement(\"h2\");\n    title.className = \"cinema-title\";\n    title.textContent = cinema.name;\n\n    const rating = document.createElement(\"span\");\n    rating.className = \"cinema-rating\";\n    rating.setAttribute(\"aria-label\", \"Rating \" + cinema.rating + \" out of 5\");\n    rating.textContent = \"★ \" + cinema.rating;\n\n    header.append(title, rating);\n\n    const location = document.createElement(\"p\");\n    location.className = \"cinema-location\";\n    location.textContent = cinema.area + \", \" + cinema.city;\n\n    const metaList = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    metaList.className = \"cinema-meta\";\n    cinema.features.forEach(function (feature) {\n      const tag = document.createElement(\"span\");\n      tag.className = \"feature-tag\";\n      tag.textContent = feature;\n      metaList.appendChild(tag);\n    });\n\n    // Footer with screen count and action button\n    const footer = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    footer.className = \"cinema-card-footer\";\n\n    const screensInfo = document.createElement(\"span\");\n    screensInfo.className = \"screens-info\";\n    screensInfo.textContent = cinema.screens + \" Screens Available\";\n\n    const selectBtn = document.createElement(\"button\");\n    selectBtn.type = \"button\";\n    selectBtn.className = \"btn btn-primary\";\n    selectBtn.textContent = \"Select Cinema\";\n    selectBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n      onSelectCinema(cinema);\n    });\n\n    footer.append(screensInfo, selectBtn);\n    body.append(header, location, metaList, footer);\n    card.append(media, body);\n\n    return card;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Initializes city dropdown, renders cinema cards, and handles selection.\n   */\n  function initSelectCinemaPage() {\n    const citySelect = document.getElementById(\"citySelect\");\n    const cinemaGrid = document.getElementById(\"cinemaGrid\");\n    const resultsCount = document.getElementById(\"resultsCount\");\n    const savedSelectionBanner = document.getElementById(\"savedSelectionBanner\");\n\n    // Only run on select-cinema.html\n    if (!citySelect || !cinemaGrid) {\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const savedState = getSavedBookingState();\n    // Default city is always Islamabad unless user already picked a valid city\n    const initialCity =\n      savedState && savedState.city ? savedState.city : DEFAULT_CITY;\n    citySelect.value = initialCity;\n\n    function updateSavedBanner() {\n      if (!savedSelectionBanner) {\n        return;\n      }\n      const latest = getSavedBookingState();\n      if (latest && latest.cinemaName) {\n        savedSelectionBanner.hidden = false;\n        savedSelectionBanner.textContent =\n          \"Last selected: \" + latest.cinemaName + \" (\" + latest.city + \")\";\n      } else {\n        savedSelectionBanner.hidden = true;\n      }\n    }\n\n    function handleCinemaSelect(cinema) {\n      saveSelectedCinema(citySelect.value, cinema);\n      window.location.href = \"show-timings.html\";\n    }\n\n    function renderCinemasForCity(selectedCity) {\n      const matchingCinemas = CINEMAS_DATA.filter(function (cinema) {\n        return cinema.city === selectedCity;\n      });\n\n      // Clear previous cards before rendering the new list\n      cinemaGrid.innerHTML = \"\";\n\n      if (resultsCount) {\n        const label = matchingCinemas.length === 1 ? \"cinema\" : \"cinemas\";\n        resultsCount.textContent =\n          \"Showing \" + matchingCinemas.length + \" \" + label + \" in \" + selectedCity;\n      }\n\n {"step_index":31,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T12:17:38Z","model":"gemini-4-hydrogen-high","input_tokens":2367,"cache_read_tokens":32390,"output_tokens":991,"content":"The base **CineVerse** project structure and `select-cinema.html` page have been created using semantic HTML5, responsive CSS3 (Flexbox & Grid), and vanilla JavaScript.\n\n### Project Structure Created\n- **Pages**\n  - [index.html](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/index.html) — Entry landing page with navigation and booking overview.\n  - [pages/select-cinema.html](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/pages/select-cinema.html) — Cinema selection page with step indicator, city selector (`Islamabad` default), dynamic cinema cards, distance badges, and mobile hamburger menu.\n  - [pages/show-timings.html](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/pages/show-timings.html) — Starter page that reads and displays the saved city and cinema from `localStorage`.\n  - [pages/seat-selection.html](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/pages/seat-selection.html) — Starter page for seat selection.\n- **Styles & Scripts**\n  - [css/style.css](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/css/style.css) — CSS custom properties, Flexbox navbar + mobile hamburger drawer, CSS Grid cinema cards, and tablet/mobile breakpoints (`992px` and `768px`).\n  - [js/booking.js](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/js/booking.js) — Well-commented vanilla JS with:\n    - `CINEMAS_DATA` array containing **Cineplex Blue Area** (`2.4 km away`), **Centaurus Cinema** (`4.1 km away`), and **F-9 Arena** (`5.8 km away`) under `Islamabad`, plus sample cinemas for `Rawalpindi`, `Lahore`, and `Karachi`.\n    - `initMobileMenu()` for accessible mobile menu toggling (`aria-expanded`, link click, and `Escape` key support).\n    - `saveSelectedCinema()` saving `cineverse_booking_state`, `cineverse_selected_city`, and `cineverse_selected_cinema` to `localStorage` before redirecting to `show-timings.html`.\n- **Assets & Logs**\n  - Local cinema illustrations in `images/`: [cineplex-blue-area.svg](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/images/cineplex-blue-area.svg), [centaurus-cinema.svg](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/images/centaurus-cinema.svg), [f9-arena.svg](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/images/f9-arena.svg), and [cinema-default.svg](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/images/cinema-default.svg).\n  - [docs/ai-logs/i263064/session-log.md](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/docs/ai-logs/i263064/session-log.md) — Development log for this setup.\n\n### Verification\n- Verified all directories and files exist and ran `node --check js/booking.js` (when `node` is present) and `git status -s` with exit code `0`. You can open [pages/select-cinema.html](file:///C:/Users/Lenovo/Desktop/IICT%20Project/A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse/pages/select-cinema.html) directly in your browser to test city filtering, cinema selection, `localStorage` persistence, and responsive layout."}

Project\\\\A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse\\\\images\\\\cinema-default.svg\"}},{\"name\":\"write_file\",\"args\":{\"Content\":\"/* ==========================================================================\\n   CineVerse - Main Stylesheet\\n   Semantic layout using CSS3 Flexbox, Grid, and responsive media queries.\\n   ========================================================================== */\\n\\n/* 1. CSS Custom Properties (Theme Variables) */\\n:root {\\n  --color-bg: #0b0f19;\\n  --color-bg-elevated: #121826;\\n  --color-surface: #161f33;\\n  --color-surface-hover: #1d2842;\\n  --color-border: #26324d;\\n  --color-text: #f1f5f9;\\n  --color-text-muted: #94a3b8;\\n  --color-primary: #e50914;\\n  --color-primary-hover: #f41b26;\\n  --color-accent: #f5c518;\\n  --color-success: #10b981;\\n\\n  --radius-sm: 8px;\\n  --radius-md: 12px;\\n  --radius-lg: 18px;\\n  --shadow-card: 0 12px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);\\n  --transition-fast: 0.2s ease;\\n  --max-width: 1200px;\\n}\\n\\n/* 2. Base Reset & Typography */\\n*,\\n*::before,\\n*::after {\\n  box-sizing: border-box;\\n  margin: 0;\\n  padding: 0;\\n}\\n\\nhtml {\\n  scroll-behavior: smooth;\\n}\\n\\nbody {\\n  font-family: \\\"Segoe UI\\\", system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, Roboto, sans-serif;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg);\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  line-height: 1.6;\\n  min-height: 100vh;\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n}\\n\\nimg {\\n  max-width: 100%;\\n  display: block;\\n}\\n\\na {\\n  color: inherit;\\n  text-decoration: none;\\n}\\n\\nbutton,\\nselect {\\n  font-family: inherit;\\n}\\n\\n.container {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  max-width: var(--max-width);\\n  margin: 0 auto;\\n  padding: 0 1.25rem;\\n}\\n\\n.sr-only {\\n  position: absolute;\\n  width: 1px;\\n  height: 1px;\\n  padding: 0;\\n  margin: -1px;\\n  overflow: hidden;\\n  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);\\n  white-space: nowrap;\\n  border: 0;\\n}\\n\\n/* 3. Header & Responsive Navigation (Flexbox + Mobile Hamburger) */\\n.site-header {\\n  background-color: rgba(11, 15, 25, 0.94);\\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  position: sticky;\\n  top: 0;\\n  z-index: 100;\\n  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);\\n}\\n\\n.navbar {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  min-height: 70px;\\n  position: relative;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-logo {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.65rem;\\n  font-size: 1.35rem;\\n  font-weight: 800;\\n  letter-spacing: 0.5px;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-badge {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  width: 36px;\\n  height: 36px;\\n  border-radius: 10px;\\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-primary), #b20710);\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n  font-size: 1.05rem;\\n  font-weight: 900;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-highlight {\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n}\\n\\n/* Hamburger button (visible on mobile) */\\n.hamburger-btn {\\n  display: none;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  gap: 5px;\\n  width: 42px;\\n  height: 42px;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  cursor: pointer;\\n  padding: 0 9px;\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-line {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  height: 2px;\\n  background-color: var(--color-text);\\n  border-radius: 2px;\\n  transition: transform var(--transition-fast), opacity var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(1) {\\n  transform: translateY(7px) rotate(45deg);\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(2) {\\n  opacity: 0;\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(3) {\\n  transform: translateY(-7px) rotate(-45deg);\\n}\\n\\n.nav-links {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  list-style: none;\\n  gap: 0.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.nav-link {\\n  display: inline-block;\\n  padding: 0.5rem 0.9rem;\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  font-weight: 500;\\n  transition: color var(--transition-fast), background-color var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.nav-link:hover,\\n.nav-link:focus-visible {\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n}\\n\\n.nav-link.active {\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\\n}\\n\\n/* 4. Main Content & Booking Progress Stepper */\\n.main-content {\\n  flex: 1;\\n  padding: 2rem 0 3.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.booking-steps {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  margin-bottom: 1.75rem;\\n  padding: 0.9rem 1.1rem;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n}\\n\\n.step-item {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.5rem;\\n  font-size: 0.92rem;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n.step-number {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  width: 26px;\\n  height: 26px;\\n  border-radius: 50%;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\\n  font-weight: 700;\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.active {\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.active .step-number {\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  border-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.completed {\\n  color: var(--color-success);\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.completed .step-number {\\n  background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.18);\\n  border-color: var(--color-success);\\n  color: var(--color-success);\\n}\\n\\n.step-divider {\\n  color: var(--color-border);\\n}\\n\\n/* 5. Page Hero & Filter Bar */\\n.page-hero {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  align-items: flex-end;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 1.5rem;\\n  margin-bottom: 1.75rem;\\n}\\n\\n.hero-text h1 {\\n  font-size: clamp(1.65rem, 3vw, 2.3rem);\\n  line-height: 1.2;\\n  margin-bottom: 0.45rem;\\n}\\n\\n.hero-text p {\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  max-width: 620px;\\n}\\n\\n.city-filter-box {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  gap: 0.4rem;\\n  min-width: 230px;\\n}\\n\\n.city-filter-label {\\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  letter-spacing: 0.5px;\\n}\\n\\n.city-select {\\n  appearance: none;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  padding: 0.7rem 2.25rem 0.7rem 0.95rem;\\n  font-size: 1rem;\\n  font-weight: 500;\\n  cursor: pointer;\\n  background-image: linear-gradient(45deg, transparent 50%, var(--color-text-muted) 50%),\\n    linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-text-muted) 50%, transparent 50%);\\n  background-position: calc(100% - 18px) 50%, calc(100% - 12px) 50%;\\n  background-size: 6px 6px, 6px 6px;\\n  background-repeat: no-repeat;\\n  transition: border-color var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.city-select:hover,\\n.city-select:focus {\\n  outline: none;\\n  border-color: var(--color-accent);\\n}\\n\\n/* Results summary & saved selection banner */\\n.results-toolbar {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n  margin-bottom: 1.25rem;\\n}\\n\\n.results-count {\\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n.saved-selection-pill {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.5rem;\\n  padding: 0.4rem 0.85rem;\\n  border-radius: 999px;\\n  background-color: rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.12);\\n  border: 1px solid rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.35);\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  font-size: 0.88rem;\\n}\\n\\n/* 6. var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n  overflow: hidden;\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  box-shadow: ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 3. Helper Functions (Paths & localStorage)\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Resolves the correct relative image folder depending on whether the\\n   * current page lives inside `/pages/` or at the project root.\\n   */\\n  function getImageBasePath() {\\n    return window.location.pathname.includes(\\\"/pages/\\\") ? \\\"../images/\\\" : \\\"images/\\\";\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Safely reads the saved booking object from localStorage.\\n   */\\n  function getSavedBookingState() {\\n    try {\\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE);\\n      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;\\n    } catch (error) {\\n      console.warn(\\\"Unable to read booking state from localStorage:\\\", error);\\n      return null;\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Saves the selected city and cinema details to localStorage.\\n   */\\n  function saveSelectedCinema(city, cinema) {\\n    const existingState = getSavedBookingState() || {};\\n    const updatedState = {\\n      ...existingState,\\n      city: city,\\n      cinemaId: cinema.id,\\n      cinemaName: cinema.name,\\n      cinemaArea: cinema.area,\\n      distanceText: cinema.distanceText,\\n      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()\\n    };\\n\\n    try {\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE, JSON.stringify(updatedState));\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CITY, city);\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CINEMA, cinema.name);\\n    } catch (error) {\\n      console.warn(\\\"Unable to save selection to localStorage:\\\", error);\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 4. Shared Mobile Hamburger Menu\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  function initMobileMenu() {\\n    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById(\\\"hamburgerBtn\\\");\\n    const navMenu = document.getElementById(\\\"primaryNav\\\");\\n\\n    if (!hamburgerBtn || !navMenu) {\\n      return;\\n    }\\n\\n    function setMenuOpen(isOpen) {\\n      hamburgerBtn.setAttribute(\\\"aria-expanded\\\", String(isOpen));\\n      navMenu.classList.toggle(\\\"is-open\\\", isOpen);\\n    }\\n\\n    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener(\\\"click\\\", function () {\\n      const currentlyExpanded = hamburgerBtn.getAttribute(\\\"aria-expanded\\\") === \\\"true\\\";\\n      setMenuOpen(!currentlyExpanded);\\n    });\\n\\n    // Close menu when a nav link is clicked on small screens\\n    navMenu.querySelectorAll(\\\".nav-link\\\").forEach(function (link) {\\n      link.addEventListener(\\\"click\\\", function () {\\n        setMenuOpen(false);\\n      });\\n    });\\n\\n    // Close menu with Escape key for keyboard accessibility\\n    document.addEventListener(\\\"keydown\\\", function (event) {\\n      if (event.key === \\\"Escape\\\") {\\n        setMenuOpen(false);\\n      }\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 5. Select Cinema Page (pages/select-cinema.html)\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Builds a single semantic <article> card element for a cinema object.\\n   */\\n  function createCinemaCard(cinema, savedCinemaId, onSelectCinema) {\\n    const card = document.createElement(\\\"article\\\");\\n    card.className = \\\"cinema-card\\\";\\n    if (savedCinemaId === cinema.id) {\\n      card.classList.add(\\\"is-selected\\\");\\n    }\\n\\n    // Media wrapper with cinema illustration & distance badge\\n    const media = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    media.className = \\\"cinema-card-media\\\";\\n\\n    const img = document.createElement(\\\"img\\\");\\n    img.src = getImageBasePath() + cinema.imageFile;\\n    img.alt = cinema.name + \\\" cinema hall\\\";\\n    img.loading = \\\"lazy\\\";\\n\\n    const distanceBadge = document.createElement(\\\"span\\\");\\n    distanceBadge.className = \\\"distance-badge\\\";\\n    distanceBadge.textContent = cinema.distanceText;\\n\\n    media.append(img, distanceBadge);\\n\\n    // Card content body\\n    const body = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    body.className = \\\"cinema-card-body\\\";\\n\\n    const header = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    header.className = \\\"cinema-card-header\\\";\\n\\n    const title = document.createElement(\\\"h2\\\");\\n    title.className = \\\"cinema-title\\\";\\n    title.textContent = cinema.name;\\n\\n    const rating = document.createElement(\\\"span\\\");\\n    rating.className = \\\"cinema-rating\\\";\\n    rating.setAttribute(\\\"aria-label\\\", \\\"Rating \\\" + cinema.rating + \\\" out of 5\\\");\\n    rating.textContent = \\\"★ \\\" + cinema.rating;\\n\\n    header.append(title, rating);\\n\\n    const location = document.createElement(\\\"p\\\");\\n    location.className = \\\"cinema-location\\\";\\n    location.textContent = cinema.area + \\\", \\\" + cinema.city;\\n\\n    const metaList = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    metaList.className = \\\"cinema-meta\\\";\\n    cinema.features.forEach(function (feature) {\\n      const tag = document.createElement(\\\"span\\\");\\n      tag.className = \\\"feature-tag\\\";\\n      tag.textContent = feature;\\n      metaList.appendChild(tag);\\n    });\\n\\n    // Footer with screen count and action button\\n    const footer = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    footer.className = \\\"cinema-card-footer\\\";\\n\\n    const screensInfo = document.createElement(\\\"span\\\");\\n    screensInfo.className = \\\"screens-info\\\";\\n    screensInfo.textContent = cinema.screens + \\\" Screens Available\\\";\\n\\n    const selectBtn = document.createElement(\\\"button\\\");\\n    selectBtn.type = \\\"button\\\";\\n    selectBtn.className = \\\"btn btn-primary\\\";\\n    selectBtn.textContent = \\\"Select Cinema\\\";\\n    selectBtn.addEventListener(\\\"click\\\", function () {\\n      onSelectCinema(cinema);\\n    });\\n\\n    footer.append(screensInfo, selectBtn);\\n    body.append(header, location, metaList, footer);\\n    card.append(media, body);\\n\\n    return card;\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Initializes city dropdown, renders cinema cards, and handles selection.\\n   */\\n  function initSelectCinemaPage() {\\n    const citySelect = document.getElementById(\\\"citySelect\\\");\\n    const cinemaGrid = document.getElementById(\\\"cinemaGrid\\\");\\n    const resultsCount = document.getElementById(\\\"resultsCount\\\");\\n    const savedSelectionBanner = document.getElementById(\\\"savedSelectionBanner\\\");\\n\\n    // Only run on select-cinema.html\\n    if (!citySelect || !cinemaGrid) {\\n      return;\\n    }\\n\\n    const savedState = getSavedBookingState();\\n    // Default city is always Islamabad unless user already picked a valid city\\n    const initialCity =\\n      savedState && savedState.city ? savedState.city : DEFAULT_CITY;\\n    citySelect.value = initialCity;\\n\\n    function updateSavedBanner() {\\n      if (!savedSelectionBanner) {\\n        return;\\n      }\\n      const latest = getSavedBookingState();\\n      if (latest && latest.cinemaName) {\\n        savedSelectionBanner.hidden = false;\\n        savedSelectionBanner.textContent =\\n          \\\"Last selected: \\\" + latest.cinemaName + \\\" (\\\" + latest.city + \\\")\\\";\\n      } else {\\n        savedSelectionBanner.hidden = true;\\n      }\\n    }\\n\\n    function handleCinemaSelect(cinema) {\\n      saveSelectedCinema(citySelect.value, cinema);\\n      window.location.href = \\\"show-timings.html\\\";\\n    }\\n\\n    function"}

data.\n\n","tool_calls":[{"name":"write_file","args":{"Content":"import json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\ntranscript_path = Path(\n    r\"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\.gemini\\antigravity-cli\\brain\\d270f425-7c52-4640-a79d-96eb50a0c8ac\\.system_generated\\logs\\transcript_full.jsonl\"\n)\noutput_path = Path(\n    r\"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Desktop\\IICT Project\\A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse\\docs\\ai-logs\\i263064\\session-01.md\"\n)\n\n\ndef make_fence(text: str, lang: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Return a markdown code fence longer than any backtick run in text.\"\"\"\n    max_run = 3\n    cur = 0\n    for ch in text:\n        if ch == \"`\":\n            cur += 1\n            if cur > max_run:\n                max_run = cur\n        else:\n            cur = 0\n    fence = \"`\" * (max_run + 1)\n    return f\"{fence}{lang}\\n{text}\\n{fence}\"\n\n\nlines = transcript_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()\nout = [\n    \"# CineVerse Session 01 — Full Verbatim Transcript\",\n    \"\",\n    \"- **Conversation ID:** `d270f425-7c52-4640-a79d-96eb50a0c8ac`\",\n    \"- **Source:** `transcript_full.jsonl` (verbatim, unedited)\",\n    \"\",\n]\n\ncount = 0\nfor raw_line in lines:\n    if not raw_line.strip():\n        continue\n    entry = json.loads(raw_line)\n    count += 1\n\n    out.append(\"---\")\n    out.append(\"\")\n    out.append(f\"## Step {entry.get('step_index')} — {entry.get('source')} / {entry.get('type')}\")\n    out.append(\"\")\n    if entry.get(\"created_at\"):\n        out.append(f\"- **Timestamp:** {entry['created_at']}\")\n    if entry.get(\"model\"):\n        out.append(f\"- **Model:** {entry['model']}\")\n    out.append(\"\")\n\n    thinking = entry.get(\"thinking\")\n    if thinking:\n        out.append(\"### Thinking\")\n        out.append(\"\")\n        out.append(make_fence(thinking, \"text\"))\n        out.append(\"\")\n\n    content = entry.get(\"content\")\n    if content:\n        out.append(\"### Content\")\n        out.append(\"\")\n        out.append(make_fence(content, \"text\"))\n        out.append(\"\")\n\n    tool_calls = entry.get(\"tool_calls\")\n    if tool_calls:\n        out.append(\"### Tool Calls\")\n        out.append(\"\")\n        for tc in tool_calls:\n            out.append(f\"#### `{tc.get('name')}`\")\n            out.append(\"\")\n            args_json = json.dumps(tc.get(\"args\"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n            out.append(make_fence(args_json, \"json\"))\n            out.append(\"\")\n\noutput_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\noutput_path.write_text(\"\\n\".join(out) + \"\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\nprint(f\"Exported {count} entries ({output_path.stat().st_size} bytes) to ==========================================================================\\n   CineVerse - Main Stylesheet\\n   Semantic layout using CSS3 Flexbox, Grid, and responsive media queries.\\n   ========================================================================== */\\n\\n/* 1. CSS Custom Properties (Theme Variables) */\\n:root {\\n  --color-bg: #0b0f19;\\n  --color-bg-elevated: #121826;\\n  --color-surface: #161f33;\\n  --color-surface-hover: #1d2842;\\n  --color-border: #26324d;\\n  --color-text: #f1f5f9;\\n  --color-text-muted: #94a3b8;\\n  --color-primary: #e50914;\\n  --color-primary-hover: #f41b26;\\n  --color-accent: #f5c518;\\n  --color-success: #10b981;\\n\\n  --radius-sm: 8px;\\n  --radius-md: 12px;\\n  --radius-lg: 18px;\\n  --shadow-card: 0 12px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);\\n  --transition-fast: 0.2s ease;\\n  --max-width: 1200px;\\n}\\n\\n/* 2. Base Reset & Typography */\\n*,\\n*::before,\\n*::after {\\n  box-sizing: border-box;\\n  margin: 0;\\n  padding: 0;\\n}\\n\\nhtml {\\n  scroll-behavior: smooth;\\n}\\n\\nbody {\\n  font-family: \\\"Segoe UI\\\", system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, Roboto, sans-serif;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg);\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  line-height: 1.6;\\n  min-height: 100vh;\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n}\\n\\nimg {\\n  max-width: 100%;\\n  display: block;\\n}\\n\\na {\\n  color: inherit;\\n  text-decoration: none;\\n}\\n\\nbutton,\\nselect {\\n  font-family: inherit;\\n}\\n\\n.container {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  max-width: var(--max-width);\\n  margin: 0 auto;\\n  padding: 0 1.25rem;\\n}\\n\\n.sr-only {\\n  position: absolute;\\n  width: 1px;\\n  height: 1px;\\n  padding: 0;\\n  margin: -1px;\\n  overflow: hidden;\\n  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);\\n  white-space: nowrap;\\n  border: 0;\\n}\\n\\n/* 3. Header & Responsive Navigation (Flexbox + Mobile Hamburger) */\\n.site-header {\\n  background-color: rgba(11, 15, 25, 0.94);\\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  position: sticky;\\n  top: 0;\\n  z-index: 100;\\n  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);\\n}\\n\\n.navbar {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  min-height: 70px;\\n  position: relative;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-logo {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.65rem;\\n  font-size: 1.35rem;\\n  font-weight: 800;\\n  letter-spacing: 0.5px;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-badge {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  width: 36px;\\n  height: 36px;\\n  border-radius: 10px;\\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-primary), #b20710);\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n  font-size: 1.05rem;\\n  font-weight: 900;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-highlight {\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n}\\n\\n/* Hamburger button (visible on mobile) */\\n.hamburger-btn {\\n  display: none;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  gap: 5px;\\n  width: 42px;\\n  height: 42px;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  cursor: pointer;\\n  padding: 0 9px;\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-line {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  height: 2px;\\n  background-color: var(--color-text);\\n  border-radius: 2px;\\n  transition: transform var(--transition-fast), opacity var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(1) {\\n  transform: translateY(7px) rotate(45deg);\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(2) {\\n  opacity: 0;\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(3) {\\n  transform: translateY(-7px) rotate(-45deg);\\n}\\n\\n.nav-links {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  list-style: none;\\n  gap: 0.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.nav-link {\\n  display: inline-block;\\n  padding: 0.5rem 0.9rem;\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  font-weight: 500;\\n  transition: color var(--transition-fast), background-color var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.nav-link:hover,\\n.nav-link:focus-visible {\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n}\\n\\n.nav-link.active {\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\\n}\\n\\n/* 4. Main Content & Booking Progress Stepper */\\n.main-content {\\n  flex: 1;\\n  padding: 2rem 0 3.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.booking-steps {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  margin-bottom: 1.75rem;\\n  padding: 0.9rem 1.1rem;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n}\\n\\n.step-item {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.5rem;\\n  font-size: 0.92rem;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n.step-number {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  width: 26px;\\n  height: 26px;\\n  border-radius: 50%;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\\n  font-weight: 700;\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.active {\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.active .step-number {\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  border-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.completed {\\n  color: var(--color-success);\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.completed .step-number {\\n  background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.18);\\n  border-color: var(--color-success);\\n  color: var(--color-success);\\n}\\n\\n.step-divider {\\n  color: var(--color-border);\\n}\\n\\n/* 5. Page Hero & Filter Bar */\\n.page-hero {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  align-items: flex-end;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 1.5rem;\\n  margin-bottom: 1.75rem;\\n}\\n\\n.hero-text h1 {\\n  font-size: clamp(1.65rem, 3vw, 2.3rem);\\n  line-height: 1.2;\\n  margin-bottom: 0.45rem;\\n}\\n\\n.hero-text p {\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  max-width: 620px;\\n}\\n\\n.city-filter-box {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  gap: 0.4rem;\\n  min-width: 230px;\\n}\\n\\n.city-filter-label {\\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  letter-spacing: 0.5px;\\n}\\n\\n.city-select {\\n  appearance: none;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  padding: 0.7rem 2.25rem 0.7rem 0.95rem;\\n  font-size: 1rem;\\n  font-weight: 500;\\n  cursor: pointer;\\n  background-image: linear-gradient(45deg, transparent 50%, var(--color-text-muted) 50%),\\n    linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-text-muted) 50%, transparent 50%);\\n  background-position: calc(100% - 18px) 50%, calc(100% - 12px) 50%;\\n  background-size: 6px 6px, 6px 6px;\\n  background-repeat: no-repeat;\\n  transition: border-color var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.city-select:hover,\\n.city-select:focus {\\n  outline: none;\\n  border-color: var(--color-accent);\\n}\\n\\n/* Results summary & saved selection banner */\\n.results-toolbar {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n  margin-bottom: 1.25rem;\\n}\\n\\n.results-count {\\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n.saved-selection-pill {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.5rem;\\n  padding: 0.4rem 0.85rem;\\n  border-radius: 999px;\\n  background-color: rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.12);\\n  border: 1px solid rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.35);\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  font-size: 0.88rem;\\n}\\n\\n/* 6. Cinema Cards Grid (CSS Grid) */\\n.cinema-grid {\\n  display: grid;\\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));\\n  gap: 1.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card {\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n  overflow: hidden;\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-card);\\n  transition: transform var(--transition-fast), border-color var(--transition-fast),\\n    background-color var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card:hover {\\n  transform: translateY(-4px);\\n  border-color: var(--color-accent);\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface-hover);\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card.is-selected {\\n  border-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(229, 9, 20, 0.35), var(--shadow-card);\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card-media {\\n  position: relative;\\n  aspect-ratio: 16 / 9;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\\n  overflow: hidden;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card-media img {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  height: 100%;\\n  object-fit: cover;\\n}\\n\\n.distance-badge {\\n  position: absolute;\\n  top: 0.75rem;\\n  right: 0.75rem;\\n  background-color: rgba(11, 15, 25, 0.88);\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  border: 1px solid rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.4);\\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.65rem;\\n  border-radius: 999px;\\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card-body {\\n  padding: 1.2rem;\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  flex: 1;\\n  gap: 0.8rem;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card-header {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: flex-start;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-title {\\n  font-size: 1.18rem;\\n  font-weight: 700;\\n  line-height: 1.3;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-rating {\\n  flex-shrink: 0;\\n  background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.14);\\n  color: var(--color-success);\\n  border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);\\n  padding: 0.18rem 0.5rem;\\n  border-radius: 6px;\\n  font-size: 0.82rem;\\n  font-weight: 700;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-location {\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  font-size: 0.92rem;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-meta {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  gap: 0.45rem;\\n}\\n\\n.feature-tag {\\n  font-size: 0.78rem;\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n  padding: 0.22rem 0.55rem;\\n  border-radius: 6px;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  color: #cbd5e1;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card-footer {\\n  margin-top: auto;\\n  padding-top: 0.65rem;\\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n}\\n\\n.screens-info {\\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n/* 7. Buttons */\\n.btn {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  gap: 0.4rem;\\n  padding: 0.65rem 1.15rem;\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  border: none;\\n  font-size: 0.94rem;\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n  cursor: pointer;\\n  transition: background-color var(--transition-fast), transform var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.btn-primary {\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n}\\n\\n.btn-primary:hover,\\n.btn-primary:focus-visible {\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary-hover);\\n}\\n\\n.btn-secondary {\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n}\\n\\n.btn-secondary:hover,\\n.btn-secondary:focus-visible {\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface-hover);\\n}\\n\\n/* 8. Home Landing & Starter Page Sections */\\n.landing-banner {\\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #161f33 0%, #0d1322 65%, #2a0c13 100%);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);\\n  padding: 2.5rem 2rem;\\n  display: grid;\\n  grid-template-columns: 1.4fr 1fr;\\n  gap: 2rem;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\\n}\\n\\n.landing-eyebrow {\\n  display: inline-block;\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\\n  font-weight: 700;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  letter-spacing: 1px;\\n  margin-bottom: 0.6rem;\\n}\\n\\n.landing-banner h1 {\\n  font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.6vw, 2.75rem);\\n  line-height: 1.15;\\n  margin-bottom: 0.85rem;\\n}\\n\\n.landing-banner p {\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  margin-bottom: 1.4rem;\\n  max-width: 56ch;\\n}\\n\\n.landing-actions {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  gap: 0.85rem;\\n}\\n\\n.info-panel {\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n  padding: 1.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.info-panel h2 {\\n  font-size: 1.25rem;\\n  margin-bottom: 0.65rem;\\n}\\n\\n.info-panel p {\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n.starter-grid {\\n  display: grid;\\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));\\n  gap: 1.25rem;\\n  margin-top: 1.25rem;\\n}\\n\\n.empty-state {\\n  grid-column: 1 / -1;\\n  text-align: center;\\n  padding: 2.5rem 1.5rem;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px dashed var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n/* 9. Footer */\\n.site-footer {\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  padding: 1.4rem 0;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\\n}\\n\\n.footer-inner {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n}\\n\\n/* 10. ==========================================================================\\n   CineVerse - Main Stylesheet\\n   Semantic layout using CSS3 Flexbox, Grid, and responsive media queries.\\n   ========================================================================== */\\n\\n/* 1. CSS Custom Properties (Theme Variables) */\\n:root {\\n  --color-bg: #0b0f19;\\n  --color-bg-elevated: #121826;\\n  --color-surface: #161f33;\\n  --color-surface-hover: #1d2842;\\n  --color-border: #26324d;\\n  --color-text: #f1f5f9;\\n  --color-text-muted: #94a3b8;\\n  --color-primary: #e50914;\\n  --color-primary-hover: #f41b26;\\n  --color-accent: #f5c518;\\n  --color-success: #10b981;\\n\\n  --radius-sm: 8px;\\n  --radius-md: 12px;\\n  --radius-lg: 18px;\\n  --shadow-card: 0 12px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);\\n  --transition-fast: 0.2s ease;\\n  --max-width: 1200px;\\n}\\n\\n/* 2. Base Reset & Typography */\\n*,\\n*::before,\\n*::after {\\n  box-sizing: border-box;\\n  margin: 0;\\n  padding: 0;\\n}\\n\\nhtml {\\n  scroll-behavior: smooth;\\n}\\n\\nbody {\\n  font-family: \\\"Segoe UI\\\", system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, Roboto, sans-serif;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg);\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  line-height: 1.6;\\n  min-height: 100vh;\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n}\\n\\nimg {\\n  max-width: 100%;\\n  display: block;\\n}\\n\\na {\\n  color: inherit;\\n  text-decoration: none;\\n}\\n\\nbutton,\\nselect {\\n  font-family: inherit;\\n}\\n\\n.container {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  max-width: var(--max-width);\\n  margin: 0 auto;\\n  padding: 0 1.25rem;\\n}\\n\\n.sr-only {\\n  position: absolute;\\n  width: 1px;\\n  height: 1px;\\n  padding: 0;\\n  margin: -1px;\\n  overflow: hidden;\\n  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);\\n  white-space: nowrap;\\n  border: 0;\\n}\\n\\n/* 3. Header & Responsive Navigation (Flexbox + Mobile Hamburger) */\\n.site-header {\\n  background-color: rgba(11, 15, 25, 0.94);\\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  position: sticky;\\n  top: 0;\\n  z-index: 100;\\n  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);\\n}\\n\\n.navbar {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  min-height: 70px;\\n  position: relative;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-logo {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.65rem;\\n  font-size: 1.35rem;\\n  font-weight: 800;\\n  letter-spacing: 0.5px;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-badge {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  width: 36px;\\n  height: 36px;\\n  border-radius: 10px;\\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-primary), #b20710);\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n  font-size: 1.05rem;\\n  font-weight: 900;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-highlight {\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n}\\n\\n/* Hamburger button (visible on mobile) */\\n.hamburger-btn {\\n  display: none;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  gap: 5px;\\n  width: 42px;\\n  height: 42px;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  cursor: pointer;\\n  padding: 0 9px;\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-line {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  height: 2px;\\n  background-color: var(--color-text);\\n  border-radius: 2px;\\n  transition: transform var(--transition-fast), opacity var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(1) {\\n  transform: translateY(7px) rotate(45deg);\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(2) {\\n  opacity: 0;\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-btn[aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\"] .hamburger-line:nth-child(3) {\\n  transform: translateY(-7px) rotate(-45deg);\\n}\\n\\n.nav-links {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  list-style: none;\\n  gap: 0.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.nav-link {\\n  display: inline-block;\\n  padding: 0.5rem 0.9rem;\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  font-weight: 500;\\n  transition: color var(--transition-fast), background-color var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.nav-link:hover,\\n.nav-link:focus-visible {\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n}\\n\\n.nav-link.active {\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\\n}\\n\\n/* 4. Main Content & Booking Progress Stepper */\\n.main-content {\\n  flex: 1;\\n  padding: 2rem 0 3.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.booking-steps {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  margin-bottom: 1.75rem;\\n  padding: 0.9rem 1.1rem;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n}\\n\\n.step-item {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.5rem;\\n  font-size: 0.92rem;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n.step-number {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  width: 26px;\\n  height: 26px;\\n  border-radius: 50%;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\\n  font-weight: 700;\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.active {\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.active .step-number {\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  border-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.completed {\\n  color: var(--color-success);\\n}\\n\\n.step-item.completed .step-number {\\n  background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.18);\\n  border-color: var(--color-success);\\n  color: var(--color-success);\\n}\\n\\n.step-divider {\\n  color: var(--color-border);\\n}\\n\\n/* 5. Page Hero & Filter Bar */\\n.page-hero {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  align-items: flex-end;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 1.5rem;\\n  margin-bottom: 1.75rem;\\n}\\n\\n.hero-text h1 {\\n  font-size: clamp(1.65rem, 3vw, 2.3rem);\\n  line-height: 1.2;\\n  margin-bottom: 0.45rem;\\n}\\n\\n.hero-text p {\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  max-width: 620px;\\n}\\n\\n.city-filter-box {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  gap: 0.4rem;\\n  min-width: 230px;\\n}\\n\\n.city-filter-label {\\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  letter-spacing: 0.5px;\\n}\\n\\n.city-select {\\n  appearance: none;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  padding: 0.7rem 2.25rem 0.7rem 0.95rem;\\n  font-size: 1rem;\\n  font-weight: 500;\\n  cursor: pointer;\\n  background-image: linear-gradient(45deg, transparent 50%, var(--color-text-muted) 50%),\\n    linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-text-muted) 50%, transparent 50%);\\n  background-position: calc(100% - 18px) 50%, calc(100% - 12px) 50%;\\n  background-size: 6px 6px, 6px 6px;\\n  background-repeat: no-repeat;\\n  transition: border-color var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.city-select:hover,\\n.city-select:focus {\\n  outline: none;\\n  border-color: var(--color-accent);\\n}\\n\\n/* Results summary & saved selection banner */\\n.results-toolbar {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n  margin-bottom: 1.25rem;\\n}\\n\\n.results-count {\\n  font-size: 0.95rem;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n.saved-selection-pill {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.5rem;\\n  padding: 0.4rem 0.85rem;\\n  border-radius: 999px;\\n  background-color: rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.12);\\n  border: 1px solid rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.35);\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  font-size: 0.88rem;\\n}\\n\\n/* 6. Cinema Cards Grid (CSS Grid) */\\n.cinema-grid {\\n  display: grid;\\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));\\n  gap: 1.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card {\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n  overflow: hidden;\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  box-shadow: var(--shadow-card);\\n  transition: transform var(--transition-fast), border-color var(--transition-fast),\\n    background-color var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card:hover {\\n  transform: translateY(-4px);\\n  border-color: var(--color-accent);\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface-hover);\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card.is-selected {\\n  border-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px rgba(229, 9, 20, 0.35), var(--shadow-card);\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card-media {\\n  position: relative;\\n  aspect-ratio: 16 / 9;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\\n  overflow: hidden;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card-media img {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  height: 100%;\\n  object-fit: cover;\\n}\\n\\n.distance-badge {\\n  position: absolute;\\n  top: 0.75rem;\\n  right: 0.75rem;\\n  background-color: rgba(11, 15, 25, 0.88);\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  border: 1px solid rgba(245, 197, 24, 0.4);\\n  padding: 0.25rem 0.65rem;\\n  border-radius: 999px;\\n  font-size: 0.8rem;\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card-body {\\n  padding: 1.2rem;\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  flex: 1;\\n  gap: 0.8rem;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-card-header {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: flex-start;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-title {\\n  font-size: 1.18rem;\\n  font-weight: 700;\\n  line-height: 1.3;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-rating {\\n  flex-shrink: 0;\\n  background-color: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.14);\\n  color: var(--color-success);\\n  border: 1px solid rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.35);\\n  padding: 0.18rem 0.5rem;\\n  border-radius: 6px;\\n  font-size: 0.82rem;\\n  font-weight: 700;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-location {\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  font-size: 0.92rem;\\n}\\n\\n.cinema-meta {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  gap: 0.45rem;\\n}\\n\\n.feature-tag {\\n  font-size: 0.78rem;\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n  padding: 0.22rem 0.55rem;\\n  border-radius: 6px;\\n  background-color: var(--color-border);\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n}\\n\\n.screens-info {\\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n/* 7. Buttons */\\n.btn {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  gap: 0.4rem;\\n  padding: 0.65rem 1.15rem;\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  border: none;\\n  font-size: 0.94rem;\\n  font-weight: 600;\\n  cursor: pointer;\\n  transition: background-color var(--transition-fast), transform var(--transition-fast);\\n}\\n\\n.btn-primary {\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary);\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n}\\n\\n.btn-primary:hover,\\n.btn-primary:focus-visible {\\n  background-color: var(--color-primary-hover);\\n}\\n\\n.btn-secondary {\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n}\\n\\n.btn-secondary:hover,\\n.btn-secondary:focus-visible {\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface-hover);\\n}\\n\\n/* 8. Home Landing & Starter Page Sections */\\n.landing-banner {\\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #161f33 0%, #0d1322 65%, #2a0c13 100%);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);\\n  padding: 2.5rem 2rem;\\n  display: grid;\\n  grid-template-columns: 1.4fr 1fr;\\n  gap: 2rem;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  margin-bottom: 2rem;\\n}\\n\\n.landing-eyebrow {\\n  display: inline-block;\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n  font-size: 0.85rem;\\n  font-weight: 700;\\n  text-transform: uppercase;\\n  letter-spacing: 1px;\\n  margin-bottom: 0.6rem;\\n}\\n\\n.landing-banner h1 {\\n  font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.6vw, 2.75rem);\\n  line-height: 1.15;\\n  margin-bottom: 0.85rem;\\n}\\n\\n.landing-banner p {\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  margin-bottom: 1.4rem;\\n  max-width: 56ch;\\n}\\n\\n.landing-actions {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  gap: 0.85rem;\\n}\\n\\n.info-panel {\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n  padding: 1.5rem;\\n}\\n\\n.info-panel h2 {\\n  font-size: 1.25rem;\\n  margin-bottom: 0.65rem;\\n}\\n\\n.info-panel p {\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n.starter-grid {\\n  display: grid;\\n  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));\\n  gap: 1.25rem;\\n  margin-top: 1.25rem;\\n}\\n\\n.empty-state {\\n  grid-column: 1 / -1;\\n  text-align: center;\\n  padding: 2.5rem 1.5rem;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px dashed var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-md);\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n}\\n\\n/* 9. Footer */\\n.site-footer {\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg-elevated);\\n  border-top: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  padding: 1.4rem 0;\\n  color: var(--color-text-muted);\\n  font-size: 0.9rem;\\n}\\n\\n.footer-inner {\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-wrap: wrap;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  gap: 0.75rem;\\n}\\n\\n/* 10. Seats\\\", \\\"Free Parking\\\"],\\n      imageFile: \\\"f9-arena.svg\\\"\\n    },\\n    {\\n      id: \\\"bahria-cinegold-rwp\\\",\\n      name: \\\"CineGold Plex Rawalpindi\\\",\\n      city: \\\"Rawalpindi\\\",\\n      area: \\\"Bahria Town Phase 7\\\",\\n      distanceKm: 16.5,\\n      distanceText: \\\"16.5 km away\\\",\\n      screens: 5,\\n      rating: \\\"4.5\\\",\\n      features:
