Gulberg\\\",\\n      city: \\\"Lahore\\\",\\n      area: \\\"Main Boulevard, Gulberg III\\\",\\n      distanceKm: 3.2,\\n      distanceText: \\\"3.2 km away\\\",\\n      screens: 7,\\n      rating: \\\"4.8\\\",\\n      features: [\\\"IMAX\\\", \\\"4K Projection\\\", \\\"Valet Parking\\\"],\\n      imageFile: \\\"cinema-default.svg\\\"\\n    },\\n    {\\n      id: \\\"nueplex-khi\\\",\\n      name: \\\"Nueplex Cinemas DHA\\\",\\n      city: \\\"Karachi\\\",\\n      area: \\\"Khayaban-e-Jami, DHA Phase 8\\\",\\n      distanceKm: 4.9,\\n      distanceText: \\\"4.9 km away\\\",\\n      screens: 8,\\n      rating: \\\"4.7\\\",\\n      features: [\\\"Ultra Screen\\\", \\\"Dolby Atmos\\\", \\\"Cafe\\\"],\\n      imageFile: \\\"cinema-default.svg\\\"\\n    }\\n  ];\\n\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 3. Helper Functions (Paths & localStorage)\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Resolves the correct relative image folder depending on whether the\\n   * current page lives inside `/pages/` or at the project root.\\n   */\\n  function getImageBasePath() {\\n    return window.location.pathname.includes(\\\"/pages/\\\") ? \\\"../images/\\\" : \\\"images/\\\";\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Safely reads the saved booking object from localStorage.\\n   */\\n  function getSavedBookingState() {\\n    try {\\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE);\\n      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;\\n    } catch (error) {\\n      console.warn(\\\"Unable to read booking state from localStorage:\\\", error);\\n      return null;\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Saves the selected city and cinema details to localStorage.\\n   */\\n  function saveSelectedCinema(city, cinema) {\\n    const existingState = getSavedBookingState() || {};\\n    const updatedState = {\\n      ...existingState,\\n      city: city,\\n      cinemaId: cinema.id,\\n      cinemaName: cinema.name,\\n      cinemaArea: cinema.area,\\n      distanceText: cinema.distanceText,\\n      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()\\n    };\\n\\n    try {\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE, JSON.stringify(updatedState));\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CITY, city);\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CINEMA, cinema.name);\\n    } catch (error) {\\n      console.warn(\\\"Unable to save selection to localStorage:\\\", error);\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 4. Shared Mobile Hamburger Menu\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  function initMobileMenu() {\\n    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById(\\\"hamburgerBtn\\\");\\n    const navMenu = document.getElementById(\\\"primaryNav\\\");\\n\\n    if (!hamburgerBtn || !navMenu) {\\n      return;\\n    }\\n\\n    function setMenuOpen(isOpen) {\\n      hamburgerBtn.setAttribute(\\\"aria-expanded\\\", String(isOpen));\\n      navMenu.classList.toggle(\\\"is-open\\\", isOpen);\\n    }\\n\\n    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener(\\\"click\\\", function () {\\n      const currentlyExpanded = hamburgerBtn.getAttribute(\\\"aria-expanded\\\") === \\\"true\\\";\\n      setMenuOpen(!currentlyExpanded);\\n    });\\n\\n    // Close menu when a nav link is clicked on small screens\\n    navMenu.querySelectorAll(\\\".nav-link\\\").forEach(function (link) {\\n      link.addEventListener(\\\"click\\\", function () {\\n        setMenuOpen(false);\\n      });\\n    });\\n\\n    // Close menu with Escape key for keyboard accessibility\\n    document.addEventListener(\\\"keydown\\\", function (event) {\\n      if (event.key === \\\"Escape\\\") {\\n        setMenuOpen(false);\\n      }\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 5. Select Cinema Page (pages/select-cinema.html)\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Builds a single semantic <article> card element for a cinema object.\\n   */\\n  function createCinemaCard(cinema, savedCinemaId, onSelectCinema) {\\n    const card = document.createElement(\\\"article\\\");\\n    card.className = \\\"cinema-card\\\";\\n    if (savedCinemaId === cinema.id) {\\n      card.classList.add(\\\"is-selected\\\");\\n    }\\n\\n    // Media wrapper with cinema illustration & distance badge\\n    const media = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    media.className = \\\"cinema-card-media\\\";\\n\\n    const img = document.createElement(\\\"img\\\");\\n    img.src = getImageBasePath() + cinema.imageFile;\\n    img.alt = cinema.name + \\\" cinema hall\\\";\\n    img.loading = \\\"lazy\\\";\\n\\n    const distanceBadge = document.createElement(\\\"span\\\");\\n    distanceBadge.className = \\\"distance-badge\\\";\\n    distanceBadge.textContent = cinema.distanceText;\\n\\n    media.append(img, distanceBadge);\\n\\n    // Card content body\\n    const body = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    body.className = \\\"cinema-card-body\\\";\\n\\n    const header = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    header.className = \\\"cinema-card-header\\\";\\n\\n    const title = document.createElement(\\\"h2\\\");\\n    title.className = \\\"cinema-title\\\";\\n    title.textContent = cinema.name;\\n\\n    const rating = document.createElement(\\\"span\\\");\\n    rating.className = \\\"cinema-rating\\\";\\n    rating.setAttribute(\\\"aria-label\\\", \\\"Rating \\\" + cinema.rating + \\\" out of 5\\\");\\n    rating.textContent = \\\"★ \\\" + cinema.rating;\\n\\n    header.append(title, rating);\\n\\n    const location = document.createElement(\\\"p\\\");\\n    location.className = \\\"cinema-location\\\";\\n    location.textContent = cinema.area + \\\", \\\" + cinema.city;\\n\\n    const metaList = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    metaList.className = \\\"cinema-meta\\\";\\n    cinema.features.forEach(function (feature) {\\n      const tag = document.createElement(\\\"span\\\");\\n      tag.className = \\\"feature-tag\\\";\\n      tag.textContent = feature;\\n      metaList.appendChild(tag);\\n    });\\n\\n    // Footer with screen count and action button\\n    const footer = document.createElement(\\\"div\\\");\\n    footer.className = \\\"cinema-card-footer\\\";\\n\\n    const screensInfo = document.createElement(\\\"span\\\");\\n    screensInfo.className = \\\"screens-info\\\";\\n    screensInfo.textContent = cinema.screens + \\\" Screens Available\\\";\\n\\n    const selectBtn = document.createElement(\\\"button\\\");\\n    selectBtn.type = \\\"button\\\";\\n    selectBtn.className = \\\"btn btn-primary\\\";\\n    selectBtn.textContent = \\\"Select Cinema\\\";\\n    selectBtn.addEventListener(\\\"click\\\", function () {\\n      onSelectCinema(cinema);\\n    });\\n\\n    footer.append(screensInfo, selectBtn);\\n    body.append(header, location, metaList, footer);\\n    card.append(media, body);\\n\\n    return card;\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Initializes city dropdown, renders cinema cards, and handles selection.\\n   */\\n  function initSelectCinemaPage() {\\n    const citySelect = document.getElementById(\\\"citySelect\\\");\\n    const cinemaGrid = document.getElementById(\\\"cinemaGrid\\\");\\n    const resultsCount = document.getElementById(\\\"resultsCount\\\");\\n    const savedSelectionBanner = document.getElementById(\\\"savedSelectionBanner\\\");\\n\\n    // Only run on select-cinema.html\\n    if (!citySelect || !cinemaGrid) {\\n      return;\\n    }\\n\\n    const savedState = getSavedBookingState();\\n    // Default city is always Islamabad unless user already picked a valid city\\n    const initialCity =\\n      savedState && savedState.city ? savedState.city : DEFAULT_CITY;\\n    citySelect.value = initialCity;\\n\\n    function updateSavedBanner() {\\n      if (!savedSelectionBanner) {\\n        return;\\n      }\\n      const latest = getSavedBookingState();\\n      if (latest && latest.cinemaName) {\\n        savedSelectionBanner.hidden = false;\\n        savedSelectionBanner.textContent =\\n          \\\"Last selected: \\\" + latest.cinemaName + \\\" (\\\" + latest.city + \\\")\\\";\\n      } else {\\n        savedSelectionBanner.hidden = true;\\n      }\\n    }\\n\\n    function handleCinemaSelect(cinema) {\\n      saveSelectedCinema(citySelect.value, cinema);\\n      window.location.href = \\\"show-timings.html\\\";\\n    }\\n\\n    function renderCinemasForCity(selectedCity) {\\n      const matchingCinemas = CINEMAS_DATA.filter(function (cinema) {\\n        return cinema.city === selectedCity;\\n      });\\n\\n      // Clear previous cards before rendering the new list\\n      cinemaGrid.innerHTML = \\\"\\\";\\n\\n      if (resultsCount) {\\n        const label = matchingCinemas.length === 1 ? \\\"cinema\\\" : \\\"cinemas\\\";\\n        resultsCount.textContent =\\n          \\\"Showing \\\" + matchingCinemas.length + \\\" \\\" + label + \\\" in \\\" + selectedCity;\\n      }\\n\\n      if (matchingCinemas.length === 0) {\\n        const empty = document.createElement(\\\"p\\\");\\n        empty.className = \\\"empty-state\\\";\\n        empty.textContent = \\\"No cinemas found for \\\" + selectedCity + \\\" yet.\\\";\\n        cinemaGrid.appendChild(empty);\\n        return;\\n      }\\n\\n      const currentSaved = getSavedBookingState();\\n      const savedCinemaId = currentSaved ? currentSaved.cinemaId : null;\\n\\n      matchingCinemas.forEach(function (cinema) {\\n        const card = createCinemaCard(cinema, savedCinemaId, handleCinemaSelect);\\n        cinemaGrid.appendChild(card);\\n      });\\n    }\\n\\n    citySelect.addEventListener(\\\"change\\\", function (event) {\\n      renderCinemasForCity(event.target.value);\\n    });\\n\\n    updateSavedBanner();\\n    renderCinemasForCity(citySelect.value);\\n  }\\n\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 6. Starter Hydrator for show-timings.html & seat-selection.html\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  function initSummaryContext() {\\n    const summaryContainer = document.getElementById(\\\"bookingContextSummary\\\");\\n    if (!summaryContainer) {\\n      return;\\n    }\\n\\n    const savedState = getSavedBookingState();\\n    if (savedState && savedState.cinemaName) {\\n      summaryContainer.innerHTML =\\n        \\\"<strong>Selected Cinema:</strong> \\\" +\\n        savedState.cinemaName +\\n        \\\" &bull; \\\" +\\n        savedState.cinemaArea +\\n        \\\" (\\\" +\\n        savedState.city +\\n        \\\" &mdash; \\\" +\\n        savedState.distanceText +\\n        \\\")\\\";\\n    } else {\\n      summaryContainer.innerHTML =\\n        'No cinema selected yet. Please choose one on the <a href=\\\"select-cinema.html\\\" style=\\\"color: var(--color-accent); text-decoration: underline;\\\">Select Cinema</a> page.';\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 7. Bootstrap on DOMContentLoaded\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  document.addEventListener(\\\"DOMContentLoaded\\\", function () {\\n CineVerse - Main Stylesheet\\n   Semantic layout using CSS3 Flexbox, Grid, and responsive media queries.\\n   ========================================================================== */\\n\\n/* 1. CSS Custom Properties (Theme Variables) */\\n:root {\\n  --color-bg: #0b0f19;\\n  --color-bg-elevated: #121826;\\n  --color-surface: #161f33;\\n  --color-surface-hover: #1d2842;\\n  --color-border: #26324d;\\n  --color-text: #f1f5f9;\\n  --color-text-muted: #94a3b8;\\n  --color-primary: #e50914;\\n  --color-primary-hover: #f41b26;\\n  --color-accent: #f5c518;\\n  --color-success: #10b981;\\n\\n  --radius-sm: 8px;\\n  --radius-md: 12px;\\n  --radius-lg: 18px;\\n  --shadow-card: 0 12px 30px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);\\n  --transition-fast: 0.2s ease;\\n  --max-width: 1200px;\\n}\\n\\n/* 2. Base Reset & Typography */\\n*,\\n*::before,\\n*::after {\\n  box-sizing: border-box;\\n  margin: 0;\\n  padding: 0;\\n}\\n\\nhtml {\\n  scroll-behavior: smooth;\\n}\\n\\nbody {\\n  font-family: \\\"Segoe UI\\\", system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, Roboto, sans-serif;\\n  background-color: var(--color-bg);\\n  color: var(--color-text);\\n  line-height: 1.6;\\n  min-height: 100vh;\\n  display: flex;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n}\\n\\nimg {\\n  max-width: 100%;\\n  display: block;\\n}\\n\\na {\\n  color: inherit;\\n  text-decoration: none;\\n}\\n\\nbutton,\\nselect {\\n  font-family: inherit;\\n}\\n\\n.container {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  max-width: var(--max-width);\\n  margin: 0 auto;\\n  padding: 0 1.25rem;\\n}\\n\\n.sr-only {\\n  position: absolute;\\n  width: 1px;\\n  height: 1px;\\n  padding: 0;\\n  margin: -1px;\\n  overflow: hidden;\\n  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0);\\n  white-space: nowrap;\\n  border: 0;\\n}\\n\\n/* 3. Header & Responsive Navigation (Flexbox + Mobile Hamburger) */\\n.site-header {\\n  background-color: rgba(11, 15, 25, 0.94);\\n  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  position: sticky;\\n  top: 0;\\n  z-index: 100;\\n  backdrop-filter: blur(10px);\\n}\\n\\n.navbar {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: space-between;\\n  min-height: 70px;\\n  position: relative;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-logo {\\n  display: flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  gap: 0.65rem;\\n  font-size: 1.35rem;\\n  font-weight: 800;\\n  letter-spacing: 0.5px;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-badge {\\n  display: inline-flex;\\n  align-items: center;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  width: 36px;\\n  height: 36px;\\n  border-radius: 10px;\\n  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--color-primary), #b20710);\\n  color: #ffffff;\\n  font-size: 1.05rem;\\n  font-weight: 900;\\n}\\n\\n.brand-highlight {\\n  color: var(--color-accent);\\n}\\n\\n/* Hamburger button (visible on mobile) */\\n.hamburger-btn {\\n  display: none;\\n  flex-direction: column;\\n  justify-content: center;\\n  gap: 5px;\\n  width: 42px;\\n  height: 42px;\\n  background-color: var(--color-surface);\\n  border: 1px solid var(--color-border);\\n  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);\\n  cursor: pointer;\\n  padding: 0 9px;\\n}\\n\\n.hamburger-line {\\n  width: 100%;\\n  height: 2px;\\n  background-color: Cinema Data Array (rendered dynamically onto select-cinema.html)\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  const CINEMAS_DATA = [\\n    {\\n      id: name: \\\"Nueplex Cinemas DHA\\\",\\n      city: \\\"Karachi\\\",\\n      area: \\\"Khayaban-e-Jami, DHA Phase 8\\\",\\n      distanceKm: 4.9,\\n      distanceText: \\\"4.9 km away\\\",\\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 3. Helper Functions (Paths & localStorage)\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Resolves the correct relative image folder depending on whether the\\n   * current page lives inside `/pages/` or at the project root.\\n   */\\n  function getImageBasePath() {\\n    return window.location.pathname.includes(\\\"/pages/\\\") ? \\\"../images/\\\" : \\\"images/\\\";\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Safely reads the saved booking object from localStorage.\\n   */\\n  function getSavedBookingState() {\\n    try {\\n      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE);\\n      return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;\\n    } catch (error) {\\n      console.warn(\\\"Unable to read booking state from localStorage:\\\", error);\\n      return null;\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Saves the selected city and cinema details to localStorage.\\n   */\\n  function saveSelectedCinema(city, cinema) {\\n    const existingState = getSavedBookingState() || {};\\n    const updatedState = {\\n      ...existingState,\\n      city: city,\\n      cinemaId: cinema.id,\\n      cinemaName: cinema.name,\\n      cinemaArea: cinema.area,\\n      distanceText: cinema.distanceText,\\n      updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()\\n    };\\n\\n    try {\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE, JSON.stringify(updatedState));\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CITY, city);\\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CINEMA, cinema.name);\\n    } catch (error) {\\n      console.warn(\\\"Unable to save selection to localStorage:\\\", error);\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 4. Shared Mobile Hamburger Menu\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  function initMobileMenu() {\\n    const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById(\\\"hamburgerBtn\\\");\\n    const navMenu = document.getElementById(\\\"primaryNav\\\");\\n\\n    if (!hamburgerBtn || !navMenu) {\\n      return;\\n    }\\n\\n    function setMenuOpen(isOpen) {\\n      hamburgerBtn.setAttribute(\\\"aria-expanded\\\", String(isOpen));\\n      navMenu.classList.toggle(\\\"is-open\\\", isOpen);\\n    }\\n\\n    hamburgerBtn.addEventListener(\\\"click\\\", function () {\\n      const currentlyExpanded = hamburgerBtn.getAttribute(\\\"aria-expanded\\\") === \\\"true\\\";\\n      setMenuOpen(!currentlyExpanded);\\n    });\\n\\n    // Close menu when a nav link is clicked on small screens\\n    navMenu.querySelectorAll(\\\".nav-link\\\").forEach(function (link) {\\n      link.addEventListener(\\\"click\\\", function () {\\n        setMenuOpen(false);\\n      });\\n    });\\n\\n    // Close menu with Escape key for keyboard accessibility\\n    document.addEventListener(\\\"keydown\\\", function (event) {\\n      if (event.key === \\\"Escape\\\") {\\n        setMenuOpen(false);\\n      }\\n    });\\n  }\\n\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n  // 5. Select Cinema Page (pages/select-cinema.html)\\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n  /**\\n   * Builds a single semantic <article> card element for a cinema object.\\n   */\\n  function createCinemaCard(cinema, savedCinemaId, onSelectCinema) {\\n    const card = document.createElement(\\\"article\\\");\\n    card.className = \\\"cinema-card\\\";\\n Initial layout planning requires validating local storage for cinema selection parameters to handle missing state gracefully before rendering timing options.\n\nInitial inclination favored a single featured film display, but supporting multiple titles via a data array provides better flexibility. Validating date and showtime selections against persistent state ensures seamless navigation to the seating interface.\n\nPre-selecting the primary film title from the available data array prevents invalid navigation states when users omit explicit title selection. Rendering date options dynamically via script generation supports accessible state management across multiple screening days.\n\nStatic generation of time slot buttons requires predefined schedule structures, yet dynamic rendering from array data improves scalability. Enforcing validation before navigation prevents incomplete state passing, though state retention from local storage complicates fresh visits by pre-selecting previous choices unexpectedly.\n\nResetting temporal parameters during cinema updates prevents stale state persistence across different venue selections. class=\"hamburger-btn\"\n        aria-label=\"Toggle navigation menu\"\n        aria-expanded=\"false\"\n        aria-controls=\"primaryNav\"\n      >\n        <span class=\"hamburger-line\"></span>\n        <span class=\"hamburger-line\"></span>\n        <span class=\"hamburger-line\"></span>\n      </button>\n\n      <nav aria-label=\"Primary Navigation\">\n        <ul id=\"primaryNav\" class=\"nav-links\">\n          <li><a href=\"../index.html\" class=\"nav-link\">Home</a></li>\n          <li><a href=\"select-cinema.html\" class=\"nav-link\">Select Cinema</a></li>\n          <li><a href=\"show-timings.html\" class=\"nav-link active\">Show Timings</a></li>\n          <li><a href=\"seat-selection.html\" class=\"nav-link\">Seat Selection</a></li>\n        </ul>\n      </nav>\n    </div>\n  </header>\n\n  <!-- Main Content -->\n  <main class=\"main-content\" id=\"showTimingsPage\">\n    <div class=\"container\">\n      <!-- Booking Progress Indicator -->\n      <nav class=\"booking-steps\" aria-label=\"Booking progress\">\n        <div class=\"step-item completed\">\n          <span class=\"step-number\">&#10003;</span>\n          <span>Select Cinema</span>\n        </div>\n        <span class=\"step-divider\" aria-hidden=\"true\">/</span>\n        <div class=\"step-item active\" aria-current=\"step\">\n          <span class=\"step-number\">2</span>\n          <span>Show Timings</span>\n        </div>\n        <span class=\"step-divider\" aria-hidden=\"true\">/</span>\n        <div class=\"step-item\">\n          <span class=\"step-number\">3</span>\n          <span>Seat Selection</span>\n        </div>\n      </nav>\n\n      <!-- Page Heading -->\n      <section class=\"page-hero\" aria-labelledby=\"showTimingsHeading\">\n        <div class=\"hero-text\">\n          <h1 id=\"showTimingsHeading\">Choose Movie, Date &amp; Showtime</h1>\n          <p>\n            Review your chosen theater, pick a convenient date and time slot, and\n            proceed to seat selection.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      <!-- Selected Cinema Context Banner (populated from localStorage) -->\n      <section\n        id=\"selectedCinemaBanner\"\n        class=\"cinema-context-card\"\n        aria-label=\"Selected cinema details\"\n      >\n        <div class=\"cinema-context-info\">\n          <h2 href=\"select-cinema.html\" class=\"btn btn-secondary\">\n          Change Cinema\n        </a>\n      </section>\n\n      <!-- Main Two-Column Show Timings Layout -->\n      <div class=\"timings-layout\">\n        <!-- Left Column: Movie Poster & Details -->\n        <aside class=\"movie-poster-card\" aria-labelledby=\"movieTitle\">\n          <div class=\"movie-poster-frame\">\n            <img\n              id=\"moviePosterImg\"\n              src=\"../images/movie-poster.svg\"\n              alt=\"Featured movie poster\"\n            />\n          </div>\n          <div class=\"movie-details-body\">\n            <h2 id=\"movieTitle\" class=\"movie-title\">Loading movie...</h2>\n            <div id=\"movieMetaBadges\" class=\"movie-badges\"></div>\n            <p id=\"movieSynopsis\" class=\"movie-synopsis\"></p>\n          </div>\n        </aside>\n\n        <!-- Right Column: Movie Switcher, Date Selector & Time Slots -->\n        <section class=\"schedule-panel\" aria-label=\"Showtime Schedule\">\n          <!-- Now Showing Movie Selector -->\n          <div>\n            <div class=\"selector-group-header\">\n              <h2>1. Select Date</h2>\n              <span class=\"selector-hint\">Required</span>\n            </div>\n            <div id=\"dateSelector\" class=\"date-grid\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Select show date\"></div>\n          </div>\n\n          <!-- Showtime Slot Buttons (rendered from JS data) -->\n          <div>\n            <div class=\"selector-group-header\">\n              <h2>3. Select Showtime</h2>\n              <span class=\"selector-hint\">Required</span>\n            </div>\n            <div id=\"timeSlotGrid\" class=\"timeslot-grid\" role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Select showtime\"></div>\n          </div>\n\n          <!-- Live Selection Summary -->\n          <div id=\"timingSelectionSummary\" class=\"selection-summary-box\" aria-live=\"polite\">\n            <span class=\"summary-field\"><strong>Movie:</strong> <span id=\"summaryMovie\">-</span></span>\n            <span class=\"summary-field\"><strong>Date:</strong> <span id=\"summaryDate\">Not selected</span></span>\n            <span class=\"summary-field\"><strong>Showtime:</strong> <span id=\"summaryTime\">Not selected</span></span>\n          </div>\n\n          <!-- Inline Validation Message -->\n          <p id=\"timingValidationMsg\" class=\"validation-message\" role=\"alert\" hidden></p>\n\n          <!-- Navigation Actions -->\n          <div class=\"schedule-actions\">\n            <a href=\"select-cinema.html\" class=\"btn btn-secondary\">&larr; Helper Functions (Paths & localStorage)\n142:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n143: \n144:   /**\n145:    * Resolves the correct relative image folder depending on whether the\n146:    * current page lives inside `/pages/` or at the project root.\n147:    */\n148:   function getImageBasePath() {\n149:     return window.location.pathname.includes(\"/pages/\") ? \"../images/\" : \"images/\";\n150:   }\n151: \n152:   /**\n153:    * Safely reads the saved booking object from localStorage.\n154:    */\n155:   function getSavedBookingState() {\n156:     try {\n157:       const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE);\n158:       return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;\n159:     } catch (error) {\n160:       console.warn(\"Unable to read booking state from localStorage:\", error);\n161:       return null;\n162:     }\n163:   }\n164: \n165:   /**\n166:    * Saves the selected city and cinema details to localStorage.\n167:    */\n168:   function saveSelectedCinema(city, cinema) {\n169:     const existingState = getSavedBookingState() || {};\n170:     const updatedState = {\n171:       ...existingState,\n172:       city: city,\n173:       cinemaId: cinema.id,\n174:       cinemaName: cinema.name,\n175:       cinemaArea: cinema.area,\n176:       distanceText: cinema.distanceText,\n177:       updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()\n178:     };\n179: \n180:     try {\n181:       localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE, JSON.stringify(updatedState));\n182:       localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CITY, city);\n183:       localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_CINEMA, cinema.name);\n184:     } catch (error) {\n185:       console.warn(\"Unable to save selection to localStorage:\", error);\n186:     }\n187:   }\n188: \n189:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n190:   // 4. Shared Mobile Hamburger Menu\n191:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n192: \n193:   function initMobileMenu() {\n194:     const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById(\"hamburgerBtn\");\n195:     const navMenu = document.getElementById(\"primaryNav\");\n196: \n197:     if (!hamburgerBtn || !navMenu) {\n198:       return;\n199:     }\n200: \n201:     function setMenuOpen(isOpen) {\n202:       hamburgerBtn.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", String(isOpen));\n203:       navMenu.classList.toggle(\"is-open\", isOpen);\n204:     }\n205: "}

Starter Hydrator for show-timings.html & seat-selection.html\n389:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n390: \n391:   function initSummaryContext() {\n392:     const summaryContainer = document.getElementById(\"bookingContextSummary\");\n393:     if (!summaryContainer) {\n394:       return;\n395:     }\n396: \n397:     const savedState = getSavedBookingState();\n398:     summaryContainer.textContent = \"\";\n399: \n400:     if (savedState && savedState.cinemaName) {\n401:       const label = document.createElement(\"strong\");\n402:       label.textContent = \"Selected Cinema: \";\n403: \n404:       const details = document.createTextNode(\n405:         savedState.cinemaName +\n406:           \" • \" +\n407:           savedState.cinemaArea +\n408:           \" (\" +\n409:           savedState.city +\n410:           \" — \" +\n411:           savedState.distanceText +\n412:           \")\"\n413:       );\n414: \n415:       summaryContainer.append(label, details);\n416:     } else {\n417:       const promptText = document.createTextNode(\n418:         \"No cinema selected yet. Please choose one on the \"\n419:       );\n420:       const link = document.createElement(\"a\");\n421:       link.href = \"select-cinema.html\";\n422:       link.textContent = \"Select Cinema\";\n423:       link.style.color = \"var(--color-accent)\";\n424:       link.style.textDecoration = \"underline\";\n425:       const suffix = document.createTextNode(\" page.\");\n426: \n427:       summaryContainer.append(promptText, link, suffix);\n428:     }\n429:   }\n430: \n431:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n432:   // 7. Bootstrap on DOMContentLoaded\n433:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n434:   document.addEventListener(\"DOMContentLoaded\", function () {\n435:     initMobileMenu();\n436:     initSelectCinemaPage();\n437:     initSummaryContext();\n438:   });\n439: })();"}
{"step_index":109,"source":"USER_EXPLICIT","type":"USER_INPUT","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T13:24:12Z","content":"<USER_REQUEST>\n\"Build pages/show-timings.html and update js/booking.js and css/style.css to match the CineVerse theme. The function should:\n\n```js\nfunction initShowTimingsPage() {\n  // Guard: only run on show-timings.html\n  if (!document.getElementById(\"showTimingsPage\")) return;\n\n  // 1. Cinema context banner\n  const cinemaTitle = document.getElementById(\"selectedCinemaTitle\");\n  const cinemaMeta = document.getElementById(\"selectedCinemaMeta\");\n  const banner = document.getElementById(\"selectedCinemaBanner\");\n  const savedState = getSavedBookingState();\n  if (savedState && savedState.cinemaName) {\n    cinemaTitle.textContent = savedState.cinemaName;\n    cinemaMeta.textContent = savedState.cinemaArea + \" • \" + savedState.city + \" (\" + savedState.distanceText + \")\";\n  } else {\n    cinemaTitle.textContent = \"No cinema selected\";\n    cinemaMeta.textContent = \"Please go back and choose a cinema first.\";\n    banner.classList.add(\"is-warning\");\n  }\n\n  // 2. Local state tracking\n  let activeMovieId = MOVIES_DATA[0].id;\n  let activeDateId = null;\n  let activeTimeId = null;\n\n  // 3. Update poster + details panel\n  function renderMovieDetails(movieId) {\n    const movie = MOVIES_DATA.find(m => m.id === movieId) || MOVIES_DATA[0];\n    const img = document.getElementById(\"moviePosterImg\");\n    img.src = getImageBasePath() + movie.posterFile;\n    img.alt = movie.title + \" movie poster\";\n    document.getElementById(\"movieTitle\").textContent = movie.title;\n    document.getElementById(\"movieSynopsis\").textContent = movie.synopsis;\n    // Badges\n    const badgesEl = document.getElementById(\"movieMetaBadges\");\n    badgesEl.innerHTML = \"\";\n    [movie.rating, movie.duration, movie.language].forEach(function(text) {\n      const tag = document.createElement(\"span\");\n      tag.className = \"feature-tag\";\n      tag.textContent = text;\n      badgesEl.appendChild(tag);\n    });\n    document.getElementById(\"summaryMovie\").textContent = movie.title;\n  }\n\n  // 4. Update live summary\n  function updateSummary() {\n    const dateObj = SHOW_DATES.find(d => d.id === activeDateId);\n    const timeObj = SHOW_TIMINGS.find(t => t.id === activeTimeId);\n    document.getElementById(\"summaryDate\").textContent = dateObj ? dateObj.label : \"Not selected\";\n    document.getElementById(\"summaryTime\").textContent = timeObj ? timeObj.time : \"Not selected\";\n  }\n\n  // 5. Render movie switcher pills\n  const movieSwitcher = document.getElementById(\"movieSwitcher\");\n  MOVIES_DATA.forEach(function(movie) {\n    const btn = document.createElement(\"button\");\n    btn.type = \"button\";\n    btn.className = \"movie-pill-btn\" + (movie.id === activeMovieId ? \" is-active\" : \"\");\n    btn.textContent = movie.title;\n    btn.addEventListener(\"click\", function() {\n      activeMovieId = movie.id;\n      movieSwitcher.querySelectorAll(\".movie-pill-btn\").forEach(b => b.classList.remove(\"is-active\"));\n      btn.classList.add(\"is-active\");\n      renderMovieDetails(activeMovieId);\n    });\n    movieSwitcher.appendChild(btn);\n  });\n\n  // 6. Render date buttons\n  const dateSelector = document.getElementById(\"dateSelector\");\n  SHOW_DATES.forEach(function(dateObj) {\n    const btn = document.createElement(\"button\");\n    btn.type = \"button\";\n    btn.className = \"date-btn\";\n    btn.dataset.dateId = dateObj.id;\n    btn.setAttribute(\"aria-label\", dateObj.label);\n    const daySpan = document.createElement(\"span\");\n    daySpan.className = \"date-btn-day\";\n    daySpan.textContent = dateObj.day;\n    const numSpan = document.createElement(\"span\");\n    numSpan.className = \"date-btn-num\";\n    numSpan.textContent = dateObj.dateNum;\n    btn.append(daySpan, numSpan);\n    btn.addEventListener(\"click\", function() {\n      activeDateId = dateObj.id;\n      dateSelector.querySelectorAll(\".date-btn\").forEach(b => b.classList.remove(\"is-selected\"));\n      btn.classList.add(\"is-selected\");\n      document.getElementById(\"timingValidationMsg\").hidden = true;\n      updateSummary();\n    });\n    dateSelector.appendChild(btn);\n  });\n\n  // 7. Render time slot buttons\n  const timeGrid = document.getElementById(\"timeSlotGrid\");\n  SHOW_TIMINGS.forEach(function(timingObj) {\n    const btn = document.createElement(\"button\");\n    btn.type = \"button\";\n    btn.className = \"time-btn\";\n    btn.dataset.timeId = timingObj.id;\n    const labelSpan = document.createElement(\"span\");\n    labelSpan.className = \"time-btn-label\";\n    labelSpan.textContent = timingObj.time;\n    const subSpan = document.createElement(\"span\");\n    subSpan.className = \"time-btn-sub\";\n    subSpan.textContent = timingObj.hall;\n    btn.append(labelSpan, subSpan);\n    btn.addEventListener(\"click\", function() {\n      activeTimeId = timingObj.id;\n      timeGrid.querySelectorAll(\".time-btn\").forEach(b => b.classList.remove(\"is-selected\"));\n      btn.classList.add(\"is-selected\");\n      document.getElementById(\"timingValidationMsg\").hidden = true;\n      updateSummary();\n    });\n    timeGrid.appendChild(btn);\n  });\n\n  // 8. Next button — validate then save + navigate\n  document.getElementById(\"nextToSeatsBtn\").addEventListener(\"click\", function() {\n    const validationMsg = document.getElementById(\"timingValidationMsg\");\n    if (!activeDateId && !activeTimeId) {\n      validationMsg.textContent = \"Please select a date and showtime before continuing.\";\n      validationMsg.hidden = false;\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!activeDateId) {\n      validationMsg.textContent = \"Please select a date to continue.\";\n      validationMsg.hidden = false;\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!activeTimeId) {\n      validationMsg.textContent = \"Please select a showtime to continue.\";\n      validationMsg.hidden = false;\n      return;\n    }\n    // All valid — save and navigate\n    validationMsg.hidden = true;\n    const movie = MOVIES_DATA.find(m => m.id === activeMovieId);\n    const dateObj = SHOW_DATES.find(d => d.id === activeDateId);\n    const timingObj = SHOW_TIMINGS.find(t => t.id === activeTimeId);\n    saveTimingSelection(movie, dateObj, timingObj);\n    window.location.href = \"seat-selection.html\";\n  });\n\n  // Initial render\n  renderMovieDetails(activeMovieId);\n  updateSummary();\n}\n```\n\n### Step 3 — Update DOMContentLoaded bootstrap in `js/booking.js`\nChange (at line ~434):\n```js\ndocument.addEventListener(\"DOMContentLoaded\", function () {\n  initMobileMenu();\n  initSelectCinemaPage();\n  initSummaryContext();  // ← remove or keep (it safely no-ops now)\n  initShowTimingsPage(); // ← ADD THIS\n});\n```\n\n### Step 4 — Remove/guard old `initSummaryContext()`\nThe function targets `#bookingContextSummary` which no longer exists in `show-timings.html`. Helper Functions (Paths & localStorage)\n142:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n143: \n144:   /**\n145:    * Resolves the correct relative image folder depending on whether the\n146:    * current page lives inside `/pages/` or at the project root.\n147:    */\n148:   function getImageBasePath() {\n149:     return window.location.pathname.includes(\"/pages/\") ? \"../images/\" : \"images/\";\n150:   }\n151: \n152:   /**\n153:    * Safely reads the saved booking object from localStorage.\n154:    */\n155:   function getSavedBookingState() {\n156:     try {\n157:       const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE);\n158:       return raw ? Shared Mobile Hamburger Menu\n191:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n192: \n193:   function initMobileMenu() {\n194:     const hamburgerBtn = document.getElementById(\"hamburgerBtn\");\n195:     const navMenu = document.getElementById(\"primaryNav\");\n196: \n197:     if (!hamburgerBtn || !navMenu) {\n198:       return;\n199:     }\n200: \n201:     function setMenuOpen(isOpen) {\n202:       hamburgerBtn.setAttribute(\"aria-expanded\", String(isOpen));\n203:       navMenu.classList.toggle(\"is-open\", isOpen);\n204:     }\n205: \n206:     hamburgerBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n207:       const currentlyExpanded = hamburgerBtn.getAttribute(\"aria-expanded\") === \"true\";\n208:       setMenuOpen(!currentlyExpanded);\n209:     });\n210: \n211:     // Close menu when a nav link is clicked on small screens\n212:     navMenu.querySelectorAll(\".nav-link\").forEach(function (link) {\n213:       link.addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n214:         setMenuOpen(false);\n215:       });\n216:     });\n217: \n218:     // Close menu with Escape key for keyboard accessibility\n219:     document.addEventListener(\"keydown\", function (event) {\n220:       if (event.key === \"Escape\") {\n221:         setMenuOpen(false);\n222:       }\n223:     });\n224:   }\n225: \n226:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n227:   // 5. Select Cinema Page (pages/select-cinema.html)\n228:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n229: \n230:   /**\n231:    * Builds a single semantic <article> card element for a cinema object.\n232:    */\n233:   function createCinemaCard(cinema, savedCinemaId, onSelectCinema) {\n234:     const card = document.createElement(\"article\");\n235:     card.className = \"cinema-card\";\n236:     if (savedCinemaId === cinema.id) {\n237:       card.classList.add(\"is-selected\");\n238:     }\n239: \n240:     // Media wrapper with cinema illustration & distance badge\n241:     const media = document.createElement(\"div\");\n242:     media.className = \"cinema-card-media\";\n243: \n244:     const img = document.createElement(\"img\");\n245:     img.src = getImageBasePath() + cinema.imageFile;\n246:     img.alt = cinema.name + \" cinema hall\";\n247:     img.loading = \"lazy\";\n248: \n249:     const distanceBadge = document.createElement(\"span\");\n250:     distanceBadge.className = \"distance-badge\";\n251:     distanceBadge.textContent = cinema.distanceText;\n252: \n253:     media.append(img, distanceBadge);\n254: \n255:     // Card content body\n256:     const body = document.createElement(\"div\");\n257:     body.className = \"cinema-card-body\";\n258: \n259:     const header = document.createElement(\"div\");\n260:     header.className = \"cinema-card-header\";\n261: \n262:     const title = document.createElement(\"h2\");\n263:     title.className = \"cinema-title\";\n264:     title.textContent = cinema.name;\n265: \n266:     const rating = document.createElement(\"span\");\n267:     rating.className = \"cinema-rating\";\n268:     rating.setAttribute(\"aria-label\", \"Rating \" + cinema.rating + \" out of 5\");\n269:     rating.textContent = \"★ \" + cinema.rating;\n270: \n271:     header.append(title, rating);\n272: \n273:     const location = document.createElement(\"p\");\n274:     location.className = \"cinema-location\";\n275:     location.textContent = cinema.area + \", \" + cinema.city;\n276: \n277:     const metaList = document.createElement(\"div\");\n278:     metaList.className = \"cinema-meta\";\n279:     cinema.features.forEach(function (feature) {\n280:       const tag = document.createElement(\"span\");\n281:       tag.className = \"feature-tag\";\n282:       tag.textContent = feature;\n283:       metaList.appendChild(tag);\n284:     });\n285: \n286:     // Footer with screen count and action button\n287:     const footer = document.createElement(\"div\");\n288:     footer.className = \"cinema-card-footer\";\n289: \n290:     const screensInfo = document.createElement(\"span\");\n291:     screensInfo.className = \"screens-info\";\n292:     screensInfo.textContent = cinema.screens + \" Screens Available\";\n293: \n294:     const selectBtn = document.createElement(\"button\");\n295:     selectBtn.type = \"button\";\n296:     selectBtn.className = \"btn btn-primary\";\n297:     selectBtn.textContent = \"Select Cinema\";\n298:     selectBtn.addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n299:       onSelectCinema(cinema);\n300:     });\n301: \n302:     footer.append(screensInfo, selectBtn);\n303:     body.append(header, location, metaList, footer);\n304:     card.append(media, body);\n305: \n306:     return card;\n307:   }\n308: \n309:   /**\n310:    * Initializes city dropdown, renders cinema cards, and handles selection.\n311:    */\n312:   function initSelectCinemaPage() {\n313:     const citySelect = document.getElementById(\"citySelect\");\n314:     const cinemaGrid = document.getElementById(\"cinemaGrid\");\n315:     const resultsCount = document.getElementById(\"resultsCount\");\n316:     const savedSelectionBanner = document.getElementById(\"savedSelectionBanner\");\n317: \n318:     // Only run on select-cinema.html\n319:     if (!citySelect || !cinemaGrid) {\n320:       return;\n321:     }\n322: \n323:     const savedState = getSavedBookingState();\n324:     // Default city is always Islamabad unless user already picked a valid city\n325:     const initialCity =\n326:       savedState && savedState.city ? savedState.city : DEFAULT_CITY;\n327:     citySelect.value = initialCity;\n328: \n329:     function updateSavedBanner() {\n330:       if (!savedSelectionBanner) {\n331:         return;\n332:       }\n333:       const latest = getSavedBookingState();\n334:       if (latest && latest.cinemaName) {\n335:         savedSelectionBanner.hidden = false;\n336:         savedSelectionBanner.textContent =\n337:           \"Last selected: \" + latest.cinemaName + \" (\" + latest.city + \")\";\n338:       } else {\n339:         savedSelectionBanner.hidden = true;\n340:       }\n341:     }\n342: \n343:     function handleCinemaSelect(cinema) {\n344:       saveSelectedCinema(citySelect.value, cinema);\n345:       window.location.href = \"show-timings.html\";\n346:     }\n347: \n348:     function renderCinemasForCity(selectedCity) {\n349:       const matchingCinemas = CINEMAS_DATA.filter(function (cinema) {\n350:         return cinema.city === selectedCity;\n351:       });\n352: \n353:       // Clear previous cards before rendering the new list\n354:       cinemaGrid.innerHTML = \"\";\n355: \n356:       if (resultsCount) {\n357:         const label = matchingCinemas.length === 1 ? \"cinema\" : \"cinemas\";\n358:         resultsCount.textContent =\n359:           \"Showing \" + matchingCinemas.length + \" \" + label + \" in \" + selectedCity;\n360:       }\n361: \n362:       if (matchingCinemas.length === 0) {\n363:         const empty = document.createElement(\"p\");\n364:         empty.className = \"empty-state\";\n365:         empty.textContent = \"No cinemas found for \" + selectedCity + \" yet.\";\n366:         cinemaGrid.appendChild(empty);\n367:         return;\n368:       }\n369: \n370:       const currentSaved = getSavedBookingState();\n371:       const savedCinemaId = currentSaved ? currentSaved.cinemaId : null;\n372: \n373:       matchingCinemas.forEach(function (cinema) {\n374:         const card = createCinemaCard(cinema, savedCinemaId, handleCinemaSelect);\n375:         cinemaGrid.appendChild(card);\n376:       });\n377:     }\n378: \n379:     citySelect.addEventListener(\"change\", function (event) {\n380:       renderCinemasForCity(event.target.value);\n381:     });\n382: \n383:     updateSavedBanner();\n384:     renderCinemasForCity(citySelect.value);\n385:   }\n386: \n387:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n388:   // 6. Starter Hydrator for show-timings.html & seat-selection.html\n389:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n390: \n391:   function initSummaryContext() {\n392:     const summaryContainer = document.getElementById(\"bookingContextSummary\");\n393:     if (!summaryContainer) {\n394:       return;\n395:     }\n396: \n397:     const savedState = getSavedBookingState();\n398:     summaryContainer.textContent = \"\";\n399: \n400:     if (savedState && savedState.cinemaName) {\n401:       const label = document.createElement(\"strong\");\n402:       label.textContent = \"Selected Cinema: \";\n403: \n404:       const details = document.createTextNode(\n405:         savedState.cinemaName +\n406:           \" • \" +\n407:           savedState.cinemaArea +\n408:           \" (\" +\n409:           savedState.city +\n410:           \" — \" +\n411:           savedState.distanceText +\n412:           \")\"\n413:       );\n414: \n415:       summaryContainer.append(label, details);\n416:     } else {\n417:       const promptText = document.createTextNode(\n418:         \"No cinema selected yet. Please choose one on the \"\n419:       );\n420:       const link = document.createElement(\"a\");\n421:       link.href = \"select-cinema.html\";\n422:       link.textContent = \"Select Cinema\";\n423:       link.style.color = \"var(--color-accent)\";\n424:       link.style.textDecoration = \"underline\";\n425:       const suffix = document.createTextNode(\" page.\");\n426: \n427:       summaryContainer.append(promptText, link, suffix);\n428:     }\n429:   }\n430: \n431:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n432:   // 7. Bootstrap on DOMContentLoaded\n433:   // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n434:   document.addEventListener(\"DOMContentLoaded\", function () {\n435:     initMobileMenu();\n436:     initSelectCinemaPage();\n437:     initSummaryContext();\n438:   });\n439: })();"}

{"step_index":122,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T13:27:30Z","model":"claude-sonnet-4-6","input_tokens":4306,"cache_read_tokens":24176,"output_tokens":541,"tool_calls":[{"name":"edit_file","args":{"Path":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Desktop\\IICT Project\\A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse\\js\\booking.js","Replacement":"  /**\n   * Saves the selected movie, date, and showtime to localStorage.\n   * Merges into existing booking state so cinema data is never overwritten.\n   * @param {Object} movie     - Full movie object from MOVIES_DATA\n   * @param {Object} dateObj   - Date object from SHOW_DATES\n   * @param {Object} timingObj - Timing object from SHOW_TIMINGS\n   */\n  function saveTimingSelection(movie, dateObj, timingObj) {\n    const existingState = getSavedBookingState() || {};\n    const updatedState = {\n      ...existingState,\n      movieId:    movie.id,\n      movieTitle: movie.title,\n      dateId:     dateObj.id,\n      dateLabel:  dateObj.label,\n      timeId:     timingObj.id,\n      timeLabel:  timingObj.time,\n      timingHall: timingObj.hall,\n      updatedAt:  new Date().toISOString()\n    };\n    try {\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.BOOKING_STATE,  JSON.stringify(updatedState));\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_MOVIE, movie.title);\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_DATE,  dateObj.label);\n      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEYS.SELECTED_TIME,  timingObj.time);\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(\"Unable to save timing selection:\", error);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 4. Shared Mobile Hamburger Menu\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------","Target":"  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 4. Shared Mobile Hamburger Menu\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------"}}]}

{"step_index":125,"source":"MODEL","type":"PLANNER_RESPONSE","status":"DONE","created_at":"2026-11-27T13:27:39Z","model":"claude-sonnet-4-6","input_tokens":879,"cache_read_tokens":28419,"output_tokens":2506,"content":"Good. Now add `initShowTimingsPage()` before the DOMContentLoaded listener, and update that listener at the same time:","tool_calls":[{"name":"edit_file","args":{"Path":"C:\\Users\\Lenovo\\Desktop\\IICT Project\\A_GrpNo.3_CineVerse\\js\\booking.js","Replacement":"  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 7. Show Timings Page (pages/show-timings.html)\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n  /**\n   * Initialises all interactive UI on the Show Timings page:\n   *   - Populates the cinema context banner from localStorage\n   *   - Renders the movie-switcher pills, date buttons and showtime slots\n   *     from their respective data arrays\n   *   - Keeps a live \"selection summary\" in sync\n   *   - Validates that both a date AND a showtime have been chosen before\n   *     persisting the selection and navigating to seat-selection.html\n   */\n  function initShowTimingsPage() {\n    // Guard — only run on the page that has this root element\n    if (!document.getElementById(\"showTimingsPage\")) { return; }\n\n    // ---- 1. Cinema context banner ----------------------------------------\n    const banner      = document.getElementById(\"selectedCinemaBanner\");\n    const cinemaTitle = document.getElementById(\"selectedCinemaTitle\");\n    const cinemaMeta  = document.getElementById(\"selectedCinemaMeta\");\n    const savedState  = getSavedBookingState();\n\n    if (savedState && savedState.cinemaName) {\n      cinemaTitle.textContent = savedState.cinemaName;\n      cinemaMeta.textContent  =\n        savedState.cinemaArea + \" • \" + savedState.city +\n        \" (\" + savedState.distanceText + \")\";\n    } else {\n      // No cinema in localStorage — show a warning state\n      cinemaTitle.textContent = \"No cinema selected\";\n      cinemaMeta.textContent  = \"Please go back and choose a cinema first.\";\n      if (banner) { banner.classList.add(\"is-warning\"); }\n    }\n\n    // ---- 2. Local page state (which movie / date / time is active) --------\n    var activeMovieId = MOVIES_DATA[0].id;\n    var activeDateId  = null;\n    var activeTimeId  = null;\n\n    // ---- 3. Helper — update the live summary strip -----------------------\n    function updateSummary() {\n      var dateObj   = SHOW_DATES.find(function (d) { return d.id === activeDateId; });\n      var timeObj   = SHOW_TIMINGS.find(function (t) { return t.id === activeTimeId; });\n      document.getElementById(\"summaryDate\").textContent =\n        dateObj ? dateObj.label : \"Not selected\";\n      document.getElementById(\"summaryTime\").textContent =\n        timeObj ? timeObj.time : \"Not selected\";\n    }\n\n    // ---- 4. Helper — update poster + details when movie changes ----------\n    function renderMovieDetails(movieId) {\n      var movie = MOVIES_DATA.find(function (m) { return m.id === movieId; }) || MOVIES_DATA[0];\n\n      // Poster image\n      var img = document.getElementById(\"moviePosterImg\");\n      img.src = getImageBasePath() + movie.posterFile;\n      img.alt = movie.title + \" movie poster\";\n\n      // Title & synopsis\n      document.getElementById(\"movieTitle\").textContent   = movie.title;\n      document.getElementById(\"movieSynopsis\").textContent = movie.synopsis;\n\n      // Badges — rating, duration, language\n      var badgesEl = document.getElementById(\"movieMetaBadges\");\n      badgesEl.innerHTML = \"\";\n      [movie.rating, movie.duration, movie.language].forEach(function (text) {\n        var tag = document.createElement(\"span\");\n        tag.className   = \"feature-tag\";\n        tag.textContent = text;\n        badgesEl.appendChild(tag);\n      });\n\n      // Keep summary movie name in sync\n      document.getElementById(\"summaryMovie\").textContent = movie.title;\n    }\n\n    // ---- 5. Render movie-switcher pill buttons ----------------------------\n    var movieSwitcher = document.getElementById(\"movieSwitcher\");\n    MOVIES_DATA.forEach(function (movie) {\n      var btn = document.createElement(\"button\");\n      btn.type        = \"button\";\n      btn.className   = \"movie-pill-btn\" + (movie.id === activeMovieId ? \" is-active\" : \"\");\n      btn.textContent = movie.title;\n\n      btn.addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n        activeMovieId = movie.id;\n        // Toggle active class on all pills\n        movieSwitcher.querySelectorAll(\".movie-pill-btn\").forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.remove(\"is-active\");\n        });\n        btn.classList.add(\"is-active\");\n        renderMovieDetails(activeMovieId);\n      });\n\n      movieSwitcher.appendChild(btn);\n    });\n\n    // ---- 6. Render date selector buttons ---------------------------------\n    var dateSelector = document.getElementById(\"dateSelector\");\n    SHOW_DATES.forEach(function (dateObj) {\n      var btn = document.createElement(\"button\");\n      btn.type      = \"button\";\n      btn.className = \"date-btn\";\n      btn.dataset.dateId = dateObj.id;\n      btn.setAttribute(\"aria-label\", dateObj.label);\n\n      var daySpan = document.createElement(\"span\");\n      daySpan.className   = \"date-btn-day\";\n      daySpan.textContent = dateObj.day;\n\n      var numSpan = document.createElement(\"span\");\n      numSpan.className   = \"date-btn-num\";\n      numSpan.textContent = dateObj.dateNum;\n\n      btn.appendChild(daySpan);\n      btn.appendChild(numSpan);\n\n      btn.addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n        activeDateId = dateObj.id;\n        // Single-select: clear all, then mark this one\n        dateSelector.querySelectorAll(\".date-btn\").forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.remove(\"is-selected\");\n        });\n        btn.classList.add(\"is-selected\");\n        // Hide validation message on a valid pick\n        document.getElementById(\"timingValidationMsg\").hidden = true;\n        updateSummary();\n      });\n\n      dateSelector.appendChild(btn);\n    });\n\n    // ---- 7. Render showtime slot buttons ---------------------------------\n    var timeGrid = document.getElementById(\"timeSlotGrid\");\n    SHOW_TIMINGS.forEach(function (timingObj) {\n      var btn = document.createElement(\"button\");\n      btn.type      = \"button\";\n      btn.className = \"time-btn\";\n      btn.dataset.timeId = timingObj.id;\n\n      var labelSpan = document.createElement(\"span\");\n      labelSpan.className   = \"time-btn-label\";\n      labelSpan.textContent = timingObj.time;\n\n      var subSpan = document.createElement(\"span\");\n      subSpan.className   = \"time-btn-sub\";\n      subSpan.textContent = timingObj.hall;\n\n      btn.appendChild(labelSpan);\n      btn.appendChild(subSpan);\n\n      btn.addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n        activeTimeId = timingObj.id;\n        // Single-select\n        timeGrid.querySelectorAll(\".time-btn\").forEach(function (b) {\n          b.classList.remove(\"is-selected\");\n        });\n        btn.classList.add(\"is-selected\");\n        document.getElementById(\"timingValidationMsg\").hidden = true;\n        updateSummary();\n      });\n\n      timeGrid.appendChild(btn);\n    });\n\n    // ---- 8. \"Next\" button — validate, save, then navigate ---------------\n    document.getElementById(\"nextToSeatsBtn\").addEventListener(\"click\", function () {\n      var validationMsg = document.getElementById(\"timingValidationMsg\");\n\n      // Determine which fields are still missing\n      if (!activeDateId && !activeTimeId) {\n        validationMsg.textContent = \"Please select a date and showtime before continuing.\";\n        validationMsg.hidden = false;\n        return;\n      }\n      if (!activeDateId) {\n        validationMsg.textContent = \"Please select a date to continue.\";\n        validationMsg.hidden = false;\n        return;\n      }\n      if (!activeTimeId) {\n        validationMsg.textContent = \"Please select a showtime to continue.\";\n        validationMsg.hidden = false;\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // All selections valid — persist to localStorage and proceed\n      validationMsg.hidden = true;\n      var movie     = MOVIES_DATA.find(function (m) { return m.id === activeMovieId; });\n      var dateObj   = SHOW_DATES.find(function (d) { return d.id === activeDateId; });\n      var timingObj = SHOW_TIMINGS.find(function (t) { return t.id === activeTimeId; });\n      saveTimingSelection(movie, dateObj, timingObj);\n      window.location.href = \"seat-selection.html\";\n    });\n\n    // ---- 9. Initial render on page load ----------------------------------\n    renderMovieDetails(activeMovieId);\n    updateSummary();\n  }\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 8. Bootstrap on DOMContentLoaded\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  document.addEventListener(\"DOMContentLoaded\", function () {\n    initMobileMenu();\n    initSelectCinemaPage();\n    initSummaryContext();    // safe no-op on pages without #bookingContextSummary\n    initShowTimingsPage();   // runs only when #showTimingsPage exists\n  });","Target":"  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // 7. Bootstrap on DOMContentLoaded\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n  document.addEventListener(\"DOMContentLoaded\", function () {\n    initMobileMenu();\n    initSelectCinemaPage();\n    initSummaryContext();\n  });"}}]}
